FILE: packages/core/src/Events/Sharing/NodeShareRevoked.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Events\Sharing;

use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Vaulter\Models\Sharing\NodeShare;

/**
 * A bearer share-link was revoked (§2.7 / M2c). A revoked share never resolves
 * again (the resolver filters on revoked_at). Carries the revoked NodeShare and
 * the actor who revoked it.
 *
 * BREAKING CONTRACT (intentional, MAJOR — review P0.2 / F-P1.7-03): no longer
 * ShouldDispatchAfterCommit; dispatched SYNCHRONOUSLY INSIDE the revoke transaction so
 * the outbox/activity rows stage atomically with the revoke. Listeners MUST be
 * synchronous and idempotent — never a queued/after-commit side-effect off this event
 * directly (drive that off the durable outbox relay). See NodeGrantCreated for the full
 * rationale and the Pending-Work note on an additive after-commit signal.
 */
final readonly class NodeShareRevoked
{
    public string $eventId;

    public function __construct(
        public NodeShare $share,
        public int|string|null $actorId = null,
        ?string $eventId = null,
    ) {
        $this->eventId = $eventId ?? (string) Str::ulid();
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/core/src/Jobs/Webhooks/DeliverWebhook.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Jobs\Webhooks;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Bus\Queueable;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Queue\ShouldQueue;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Http\Client\ConnectionException;
use Illuminate\Http\Client\Response;
use Illuminate\Queue\InteractsWithQueue;
use Illuminate\Queue\SerializesModels;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Psr\Http\Message\ResponseInterface;
use Throwable;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\TraceRecorder;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Webhooks\OutboundUrlGuard;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\WebhookDelivery;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\WebhookEndpoint;
use Vaulter\Observability\TracingAttributes;
use Vaulter\Observability\TracingSpanNames;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Webhooks\PinnedOutboundEndpoint;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\ResponseSizeLimit;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\WebhookSigner;

/**
 * Delivers one WebhookDelivery to its endpoint over signed HTTP POST (master plan
 * §2.11 / D14 / M3b), ported from chatom — the reliable webhook channel. The delivery
 * is QUEUED, so it never blocks the originating request.
 *
 * At-least-once with exponential backoff and a dead-letter terminal state:
 *   - 2xx        → delivery 'delivered'; endpoint failure_count reset, touched.
 *   - non-2xx /
 *     transport  → attempts++, last_error recorded. If attempts are left, the delivery
 *     exception      is parked 'failed' with next_retry_at on the configured backoff
 *                    and the job returns; the relay tick (vaulter:relay-outbox /
 *                    vaulter:retry-webhooks) re-dispatches it once due. Otherwise it is
 *                    parked 'dead' (DLQ) and the endpoint's failure_count is bumped —
 *                    once it reaches disable_after_failures the endpoint auto-disables.
 *
 * Retry has a SINGLE owner: the relay's due-retry sweep, NOT the queue. The job never
 * self-releases, so a running worker and a scheduled relay can't both re-arm the same
 * failed row (no double-POST, no attempts race).
 *
 * A database lease excludes overlapping jobs for the same delivery. HTTP runs
 * outside a transaction; completion is fenced by the lease token. A lost worker's
 * lease expires so the retry sweep can recover it. Remote acceptance followed by
 * a worker crash still permits a repeated POST (at-least-once).
 */
final class DeliverWebhook implements ShouldQueue
{
    use InteractsWithQueue;
    use Queueable;
    use SerializesModels;

    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $deliveryId,
    ) {}

    public function handle(WebhookSigner $signer, OutboundUrlGuard $urlGuard): void
    {
        $metrics = app(MetricsRecorder::class);
        $tracer = app(TraceRecorder::class);

        $tracer->span(
            name: TracingSpanNames::WEBHOOK_DELIVERY,
            operation: function () use ($metrics, $signer, $urlGuard): void {
                $delivery = $this->resolveDelivery();

                if ($delivery === null || $this->isTerminal(delivery: $delivery)) {
                    return;
                }

                $endpoint = $delivery->endpoint;

                if (!$endpoint instanceof WebhookEndpoint) {
                    return;
                }

                if (!$this->claim(delivery: $delivery)) {
                    return;
                }

                $started = hrtime(true);
                $outcome = 'failure';

                try {
                    $response = $this->post(signer: $signer, urlGuard: $urlGuard, delivery: $delivery, endpoint: $endpoint);
                } catch (Throwable $throwable) {
                    $outcome = $throwable instanceof ConnectionException ? 'timeout' : 'failure';
                    if ($this->settle(delivery: $delivery, operation: fn () => $this->recordFailure(delivery: $delivery, endpoint: $endpoint, error: $throwable->getMessage()))) {
                        $this->recordWebhookMetrics(metrics: $metrics, started: $started, delivery: $delivery, outcome: $outcome);
                    }

                    return;
                }

                if ($response->successful()) {
                    if ($this->settle(delivery: $delivery, operation: fn () => $this->recordSuccess(delivery: $delivery, endpoint: $endpoint))) {
                        $this->recordWebhookMetrics(metrics: $metrics, started: $started, delivery: $delivery, outcome: 'success');
                    }

                    return;
                }

                if (!$this->settle(delivery: $delivery, operation: fn () => $this->recordFailure(
                    delivery: $delivery, endpoint: $endpoint, error: 'HTTP '.$response->status(),
                ))) {
                    return;
                }

                $delivery->refresh();
                $outcome = $delivery->status === 'dead' ? 'failure' : 'retry';
                $this->recordWebhookMetrics(metrics: $metrics, started: $started, delivery: $delivery, outcome: $outcome);
            },
            attributes: TracingAttributes::forTarget(targetId: 'webhook.pipeline', layer: 'job'),
        );
    }

    private function recordWebhookMetrics(
        MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        int $started,
        WebhookDelivery $delivery,
        string $outcome,
    ): void {
        $duration = (hrtime(true) - $started) / 1_000_000_000;
        $enqueuedAt = $delivery->created_at?->getTimestamp();

        if ($enqueuedAt !== null) {
            $metrics->histogram(
                name: 'vaulter_webhook_queue_wait_seconds',
                value: max(0, microtime(true) - (float) $enqueuedAt),
                labels: ['outcome' => $outcome],
            );
        }

        $metrics->histogram(
            name: 'vaulter_webhook_delivery_duration_seconds',
            value: $duration,
            labels: ['outcome' => $outcome],
        );

        if (in_array($outcome, ['success', 'failure'], true)) {
            $metrics->counter(
                name: 'vaulter_webhook_delivered_total',
                labels: ['outcome' => $outcome],
            );
        }
    }

    /**
     * POST the signed payload to the endpoint. The signature is HMAC-SHA256 over
     * "{timestamp}.{body}" with the endpoint's signing_secret, so the receiver can
     * verify it byte-for-byte AND reject a replayed request whose timestamp is stale.
     *
     * SSRF-hardened (§2.11 / N2 / F-P1.7-01): the guard resolves + range-checks the
     * URL on the delivery path and hands back the vetted IP, which is PINNED onto the
     * connection (CURLOPT_RESOLVE) so DNS cannot rebind to an internal address between
     * the check and the dial. The transport is locked to HTTP(S) and the response body
     * is capped (F-P1.7-06). A blocked URL throws and is recorded as a failed attempt —
     * nothing leaves the process.
     *
     * REDIRECTS ARE NEVER FOLLOWED (VLT-REMED P5.2 / R-05 / D30). The pin covers only the
     * ORIGINAL host:port; a redirect hop is resolved again, with no guard and no pin, so a
     * receiver answering `302 Location: http://169.254.169.254/…` walks the request straight
     * onto the metadata service and reopens the Blocker in full. This used to be a config
     * key (`webhooks.follow_redirects`) — a boolean that switched a security barrier off is
     * not flexibility, it is a mine, so the key is GONE and the behaviour is unconditional.
     * A webhook receiver is expected to answer for itself, not to bounce us elsewhere.
     */
    private function post(WebhookSigner $signer, OutboundUrlGuard $urlGuard, WebhookDelivery $delivery, WebhookEndpoint $endpoint): Response
    {
        $pinned = $urlGuard->pin($endpoint->url);

        $body = (string) json_encode($delivery->payload);
        $timestamp = now()->getTimestamp();

        $request = Http::timeout(Config::webhookTimeout())
            ->connectTimeout(Config::webhookConnectTimeout())
            // Unconditional (D30) — see the docblock: a redirect hop escapes the pin.
            ->withoutRedirecting()
            ->withOptions($this->transportOptions(pinned: $pinned))
            ->withHeaders([
                'Content-Type' => 'application/json',
                Config::webhookEventHeader() => $delivery->event,
                Config::webhookTimestampHeader() => (string) $timestamp,
                Config::webhookSignatureHeader() => $signer->sign(
                    body: $body,
                    secret: $endpoint->signing_secret,
                    timestamp: $timestamp,
                ),
            ])
            ->withBody($body, 'application/json');

        return $request->post($endpoint->url);
    }

    /**
     * The Guzzle transport options that harden a delivery (§2.11 / N2): pin the vetted
     * IP onto the connection, lock the protocol to HTTP(S), forbid the transport itself
     * from following a redirect (the second barrier under `withoutRedirecting()` — R-05:
     * a cURL handle that never follows Location cannot leave the pinned host even if the
     * HTTP layer is reconfigured), and bound the response body — an oversized declared
     * Content-Length is refused from the on_headers hook, and the buffering sink spills to
     * a temp file so an UNDECLARED oversize body never lands whole in RAM.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private function transportOptions(PinnedOutboundEndpoint $pinned): array
    {
        $options = [
            'curl' => [
                CURLOPT_RESOLVE => [$pinned->curlResolveEntry()],
                CURLOPT_PROTOCOLS => CURLPROTO_HTTP | CURLPROTO_HTTPS,
                CURLOPT_REDIR_PROTOCOLS => CURLPROTO_HTTP | CURLPROTO_HTTPS,
                CURLOPT_FOLLOWLOCATION => false,
            ],
        ];

        $maxResponseBytes = Config::webhookMaxResponseBytes();

        if ($maxResponseBytes > 0) {
            $sink = fopen('php://temp/maxmemory:'.$maxResponseBytes, 'w+b');

            if ($sink !== false) {
                $options['sink'] = $sink;
            }

            $options['on_headers'] = static function (ResponseInterface $response) use ($maxResponseBytes): void {
                $header = $response->getHeaderLine('Content-Length');

                ResponseSizeLimit::assertContentLengthWithinLimit(
                    contentLength: $header === '' ? null : $header,
                    maxBytes: $maxResponseBytes,
                );
            };
        }

        return $options;
    }

    private function recordSuccess(WebhookDelivery $delivery, WebhookEndpoint $endpoint): void
    {
        $delivery->forceFill([
            'status' => 'delivered',
            'attempts' => $delivery->attempts + 1,
            'next_retry_at' => null,
            'last_error' => null,
        ])->save();

        $endpoint->forceFill([
            'last_triggered_at' => now(),
            'failure_count' => 0,
        ])->save();
    }

    /**
     * Account a failed attempt: bump attempts, record the error, then either arm the
     * next retry (durable next_retry_at on backoff — the relay sweep re-dispatches it
     * once due, the job does NOT self-release) or, once the attempt budget is spent,
     * park the delivery dead and bump the endpoint's failure counter — auto-disabling
     * it past the configured threshold.
     */
    private function recordFailure(WebhookDelivery $delivery, WebhookEndpoint $endpoint, string $error): void
    {
        $attempts = $delivery->attempts + 1;

        if ($attempts < Config::webhookMaxAttempts()) {
            $backoff = Config::webhookBackoff(attempt: $attempts);

            $delivery->forceFill([
                'status' => 'failed',
                'attempts' => $attempts,
                'next_retry_at' => now()->addSeconds($backoff),
                'last_error' => $error,
            ])->save();

            return;
        }

        $delivery->forceFill([
            'status' => 'dead',
            'attempts' => $attempts,
            'next_retry_at' => null,
            'last_error' => $error,
        ])->save();

        $this->penalizeEndpoint(endpoint: $endpoint);
    }

    private function penalizeEndpoint(WebhookEndpoint $endpoint): void
    {
        $failures = $endpoint->failure_count + 1;

        $endpoint->forceFill([
            'failure_count' => $failures,
            'is_active' => $failures < Config::webhookDisableAfterFailures(),
        ])->save();
    }

    private function isTerminal(WebhookDelivery $delivery): bool
    {
        return in_array($delivery->status, ['delivered', 'dead'], true);
    }

    private function claim(WebhookDelivery $delivery): bool
    {
        $token = bin2hex(random_bytes(16));
        // Leave room for DNS/transport setup and settlement beyond the HTTP limit.
        $until = now()->addSeconds(max(Config::webhookRetryClaimSeconds(), Config::webhookTimeout() + 30));

        $claimed = $delivery->newQuery()
            ->whereKey($delivery->getKey())
            ->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'failed'])
            ->where('attempts', $delivery->attempts)
            ->where(static fn (Builder $query): Builder => $query->whereNull('claim_until')->orWhere('claim_until', '<=', now()))
            ->update(['claim_token' => $token, 'claim_until' => $until]);

        if ($claimed !== 1) {
            return false;
        }

        $delivery->forceFill(['claim_token' => $token, 'claim_until' => $until]);

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * @param Closure(): void $operation
     */
    private function settle(WebhookDelivery $delivery, Closure $operation): bool
    {
        return $delivery->getConnection()->transaction(function () use ($delivery, $operation): bool {
            // An atomic conditional write also fences on SQLite, where FOR UPDATE
            // is unavailable. A superseded worker cannot overwrite its successor.
            $owned = $delivery->newQuery()->whereKey($delivery->getKey())
                ->where('claim_token', $delivery->claim_token)
                ->update(['claim_token' => null, 'claim_until' => null]);

            if ($owned !== 1) {
                return false;
            }

            $delivery->forceFill(['claim_token' => null, 'claim_until' => null]);
            $operation();

            return true;
        });
    }

    private function resolveDelivery(): ?WebhookDelivery
    {
        /** @var class-string<WebhookDelivery> $deliveryClass */
        $deliveryClass = Config::webhookDeliveryModel();

        return $deliveryClass::query()->find($this->deliveryId);
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/core/src/Models/Webhooks/Outbox.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Models\Webhooks;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Vaulter\Models\Concerns\HasVaulterKeys;
use Vaulter\Models\Concerns\ResolvesVaulterConnection;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * A transactional-outbox row (master plan §2.11 / D14 / M3b): a domain event
 * durably staged for reliable webhook fan-out. event_id is UNIQUE — the SYNCHRONOUS
 * half (the OutboxRecorder) is idempotent by it, so the same event never stages a
 * duplicate row, and the relay drains each row exactly once.
 *
 * Swappable — point config('vaulter.models.outbox') at a subclass for tenant
 * scoping or extra behaviour.
 *
 * @property int|string           $id
 * @property string               $event_id
 * @property string               $panel
 * @property string|null          $topic
 * @property string               $event
 * @property array<string, mixed> $payload
 * @property Carbon               $available_at
 * @property Carbon|null          $dispatched_at
 * @property int                  $attempts
 * @property string               $status
 * @property Carbon|null          $created_at
 * @property Carbon|null          $updated_at
 *
 * @method static Builder<static> query()
 */
class Outbox extends Model
{
    use HasVaulterKeys;
    use ResolvesVaulterConnection;

    /**
     * Fully guarded (F-P1.8-02): the transactional-outbox staging row — every
     * column is system-computed (event_id, payload, status, retry bookkeeping).
     * The sole writer, OutboxRecorder::stage(), already uses forceFill()
     * exclusively.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    protected $guarded = ['*'];

    #[\Override]
    public function getTable(): string
    {
        return Config::table('outbox');
    }

    /**
     * The pending rows that are due (available_at <= now), oldest-first — the
     * canonical relay-drain order (§2.11). The relay claims a batch from this scope.
     *
     * @param  Builder<$this> $query
     * @return Builder<$this>
     */
    public function scopeDue(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query
            ->where('status', 'pending')
            ->where('available_at', '<=', now())
            ->orderBy('available_at');
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    #[\Override]
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'payload' => 'array',
            'available_at' => 'datetime',
            'dispatched_at' => 'datetime',
            'attempts' => 'integer',
        ];
    }
}

FILE: packages/core/src/Models/Webhooks/WebhookDelivery.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Models\Webhooks;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Vaulter\Models\Concerns\HasVaulterKeys;
use Vaulter\Models\Concerns\ResolvesVaulterConnection;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * A single delivery attempt of an event to a webhook endpoint, with retry
 * bookkeeping (master plan §2.11 / D14 / M3b). At-least-once with exponential
 * backoff: a non-2xx parks it 'failed' with next_retry_at on the backoff schedule;
 * the relay's due-retry sweep re-queues it; past the attempt budget it terminalises
 * 'dead' (DLQ) for inspection.
 *
 * Swappable — point config('vaulter.models.webhook_delivery') at a subclass.
 *
 * @property int|string           $id
 * @property int|string           $endpoint_id
 * @property string               $event
 * @property array<string, mixed> $payload
 * @property string               $status
 * @property int                  $attempts
 * @property Carbon|null          $next_retry_at
 * @property string|null          $claim_token
 * @property Carbon|null          $claim_until
 * @property string|null          $last_error
 * @property Carbon|null          $created_at
 * @property Carbon|null          $updated_at
 *
 * @method static Builder<static> query()
 */
class WebhookDelivery extends Model
{
    use HasVaulterKeys;
    use ResolvesVaulterConnection;

    /**
     * Fully guarded (F-P1.7-04): a delivery-attempt row — every column is
     * system-computed retry bookkeeping. The sole writer, OutboxRelay::
     * createDelivery(), already uses forceFill() exclusively.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    protected $guarded = ['*'];

    #[\Override]
    public function getTable(): string
    {
        return Config::table('webhook_deliveries');
    }

    /**
     * @return BelongsTo<WebhookEndpoint, $this>
     */
    public function endpoint(): BelongsTo
    {
        /** @var class-string<WebhookEndpoint> $related */
        $related = Config::webhookEndpointModel();

        return $this->belongsTo(related: $related, foreignKey: 'endpoint_id');
    }

    /**
     * The deliveries whose backoff window has elapsed and are still retryable
     * (status in pending|failed, next_retry_at <= now). The relay sweep re-queues
     * these; terminal rows (delivered|dead) are excluded.
     *
     * @param  Builder<$this> $query
     * @return Builder<$this>
     */
    public function scopeDueForRetry(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query
            ->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'failed'])
            ->whereNotNull('next_retry_at')
            ->where('next_retry_at', '<=', now())
            ->where(static fn (Builder $claim): Builder => $claim
                ->whereNull('claim_until')
                ->orWhere('claim_until', '<=', now()));
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    #[\Override]
    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'payload' => 'array',
            'attempts' => 'integer',
            'next_retry_at' => 'datetime',
            'claim_until' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }
}

FILE: packages/core/src/Webhooks/OutboxRecorder.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Webhooks;

use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Enums\Webhooks\WebhookEvent;
use Vaulter\Events\Access\NodeGrantCreated;
use Vaulter\Events\Access\NodeGrantRevoked;
use Vaulter\Events\Sharing\NodeShareCreated;
use Vaulter\Events\Sharing\NodeShareRevoked;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\Outbox;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;

/**
 * The SYNCHRONOUS half of the transactional outbox (master plan §2.11 / D14 / M3b),
 * ported from chatom's WebhookDispatcher. It maps a known Vaulter domain event into a
 * SLIM webhook payload and durably stages it as ONE v_outbox row — idempotent by
 * event_id, so a re-fired event never stages a duplicate. The actual HTTP delivery
 * (retries, backoff, DLQ, signed POST) is the relay's job.
 *
 * Kernel domains (Access grants, Sharing links) map their own events here; the Drive
 * behaviour package (P2 #5 / ADR-0015) maps its file/folder/upload events through the
 * PUBLIC {@see stage()} seam, so core no longer references any Vaulter\Drive\Events\*
 * class while the outbox payloads retain their domain fields.
 *
 * Slim payloads (HARDENING): only ids, names, sizes and enum values travel — never
 * blob bytes or secrets (a share's token_hash/secret is never serialized; only the
 * non-secret token_prefix). The event_id is STABLE and event-derived so the relay
 * and downstream consumers can dedupe at-least-once delivery on it.
 *
 * Thin side-effect service (store-only): it persists an outbox row and nothing else.
 * It makes no business decisions and never accepts a Request. Not final — a host may
 * subclass it to enrich payloads, and the subscriber's resilience is test-mockable.
 */
class OutboxRecorder
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly MetricsRecorder $metrics,
    ) {}

    public function recordGrantCreated(NodeGrantCreated $event): ?Outbox
    {
        $node = $event->grant->node;

        return $this->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::GrantCreated,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $node->panel,
            topic: $this->id($node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'grant_id' => $this->id($event->grant->getKey()),
                'node_id' => $this->id($node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->id($node->storage_id),
                'subject_type' => $event->grant->subject_type,
                'subject_id' => $this->id($event->grant->subject_id),
                'level' => $event->grant->level->value,
                'effect' => $event->grant->effect->value,
                'scope' => $event->grant->scope->value,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        );
    }

    public function recordGrantRevoked(NodeGrantRevoked $event): ?Outbox
    {
        return $this->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::GrantRevoked,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->node->panel,
            topic: $this->id($event->node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->id($event->node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->id($event->node->storage_id),
                'subject_type' => $event->subject->type,
                'subject_id' => $this->id($event->subject->id),
                'revoked' => $event->revoked,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        );
    }

    public function recordShareCreated(NodeShareCreated $event): ?Outbox
    {
        $node = $event->share->node;

        return $this->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::ShareCreated,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $node->panel,
            topic: $this->id($node->getKey()),
            // The raw token / token_hash is NEVER serialized — only the non-secret
            // token_prefix travels (slim-payload hardening).
            payload: [
                'share_id' => $this->id($event->share->getKey()),
                'node_id' => $this->id($node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->id($node->storage_id),
                'token_prefix' => $event->share->token_prefix,
                'level' => $event->share->level->value,
                'scope' => $event->share->scope->value,
                'permissions' => $event->share->permissions,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        );
    }

    public function recordShareRevoked(NodeShareRevoked $event): ?Outbox
    {
        $node = $event->share->node;

        return $this->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::ShareRevoked,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $node->panel,
            topic: $this->id($node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'share_id' => $this->id($event->share->getKey()),
                'node_id' => $this->id($node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->id($node->storage_id),
                'token_prefix' => $event->share->token_prefix,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        );
    }

    /**
     * Stage ONE outbox row for an event — the PUBLIC seam the kernel handlers above
     * and the vaulter-drive subscriber both map through. Idempotent by event_id: when
     * a row for this occurrence id already exists (a retried send, a re-fired event), this is a
     * no-op and returns null, so the relay never fans the same event out twice.
     *
     * @param array<string, mixed> $payload
     */
    public function stage(
        WebhookEvent $event,
        string $eventId,
        string $panel,
        ?string $topic,
        array $payload,
        int|string|null $actorId,
    ): ?Outbox {
        /** @var class-string<Outbox> $outboxClass */
        $outboxClass = Config::outboxModel();

        if ($outboxClass::query()->where('event_id', $eventId)->exists()) {
            return null;
        }

        return $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_outbox_stage_duration_seconds',
            operation: function () use ($outboxClass, $eventId, $panel, $topic, $event, $payload, $actorId): Outbox {
                // The actor id rides inside the payload so consumers see who caused the event
                // without a separate column on the wire.
                $payload['event_id'] = $eventId;
                $payload['actor_id'] = Identity::key($actorId);

                // forceFill (P1.4/F-P1.8-02): Outbox is intentionally fully guarded — this
                // is the outbox's own trusted staging seam, never raw request input.
                /** @var Outbox $outbox */
                $outbox = new $outboxClass;
                $outbox->forceFill([
                    'event_id' => $eventId,
                    'panel' => $panel,
                    'topic' => $topic,
                    'event' => $event->value,
                    'payload' => $payload,
                    'available_at' => now(),
                    'status' => 'pending',
                    'attempts' => 0,
                ]);
                $outbox->save();

                return $outbox;
            },
        );
    }

    /**
     * Stringify an id for a slim payload/topic, mapping the empty string to null. The
     * PUBLIC helper the vaulter-drive subscriber reuses so its payload ids match the
     * kernel handlers' exactly.
     */
    public function id(mixed $value): ?string
    {
        $string = Identity::stringify($value);

        return $string === '' ? null : $string;
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/core/src/Webhooks/OutboxRelay.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Webhooks;

use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Bus;
use Illuminate\Support\LazyCollection;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\TraceRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Jobs\Webhooks\DeliverWebhook;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\Outbox;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\WebhookDelivery;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\WebhookEndpoint;
use Vaulter\Observability\TracingAttributes;
use Vaulter\Observability\TracingSpanNames;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;

/**
 * The transactional-outbox RELAY (master plan §2.11 / D14 / M3b), ported from chatom
 * 1:1 — the reliable webhook fan-out half.
 *
 * process() drains due pending outbox rows (status='pending', available_at <= now),
 * expands each into one WebhookDelivery per subscribed ACTIVE endpoint, and dispatches
 * a DeliverWebhook job per delivery. The outbox row is then marked 'dispatched'.
 *
 * IDEMPOTENCY is row-state driven: only 'pending' rows are claimed, and the
 * read+flip-to-dispatched runs under a row lock inside a transaction, so a second
 * concurrent or repeated process() never fans the same event out twice. Combined with
 * the event_id UNIQUE staging guard, the whole pipeline is exactly-once into
 * deliveries; the HTTP delivery itself is at-least-once (consumers dedupe on event_id).
 */
final class OutboxRelay
{
    public function __construct(
        private readonly MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private readonly TraceRecorder $tracer,
        private readonly VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Drain up to $limit due pending outbox rows into per-endpoint webhook
     * deliveries and dispatch their delivery jobs. Returns the number of outbox rows
     * processed (whether or not any endpoint was subscribed).
     */
    public function process(int $limit = 100): int
    {
        return $this->tracer->span(
            name: TracingSpanNames::WEBHOOK_RELAY,
            operation: fn (): int => $this->metrics->timing(
                name: 'vaulter_outbox_relay_duration_seconds',
                operation: function () use ($limit): int {
                    $messages = $this->claimPending(limit: $limit);

                    foreach ($messages as $message) {
                        $this->fanOut(message: $message);
                        $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_outbox_relayed_total');
                    }

                    return $messages->count();
                },
            ),
            attributes: TracingAttributes::forTarget(targetId: 'webhook.pipeline', layer: 'action'),
        );
    }

    /**
     * Atomically read and flip a batch of due pending outbox rows to 'dispatched'
     * under a row lock, so the same row is never relayed by two workers. The status
     * flip guarantees a given row is fanned out once per process() call even where the
     * lock degrades (e.g. SQLite).
     *
     * @return Collection<int, Outbox>
     */
    private function claimPending(int $limit): Collection
    {
        /** @var class-string<Outbox> $outboxClass */
        $outboxClass = Config::outboxModel();

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($outboxClass, $limit): Collection {
            /** @var Collection<int, Outbox> $messages */
            $messages = $outboxClass::query()
                ->due()
                ->limit($limit)
                ->lockForUpdate()
                ->get();

            $messages->each(static function (Outbox $message): void {
                $message->forceFill([
                    'status' => 'dispatched',
                    'dispatched_at' => now(),
                ])->save();
            });

            return $messages;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Create a pending WebhookDelivery for every active endpoint subscribed to the
     * outbox row's event and dispatch its delivery job.
     */
    private function fanOut(Outbox $message): void
    {
        $scanned = 0;

        foreach ($this->subscribedEndpoints(event: $message->event) as $endpoint) {
            $scanned++;
            $delivery = $this->createDelivery(message: $message, endpoint: $endpoint);

            Bus::dispatch(new DeliverWebhook(deliveryId: Identity::stringify($delivery->getKey())));
        }

        $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_outbox_endpoints_scanned_total', amount: $scanned);
    }

    /**
     * Read only matching active endpoint IDs in key order and bounded pages. Laravel
     * compiles the JSON membership predicate for each supported database driver.
     *
     * @return LazyCollection<int, WebhookEndpoint>
     */
    private function subscribedEndpoints(string $event): LazyCollection
    {
        /** @var class-string<WebhookEndpoint> $endpointClass */
        $endpointClass = Config::webhookEndpointModel();

        $key = (new $endpointClass)->getKeyName();

        return $endpointClass::query()
            ->active()
            ->whereJsonContains('events', $event)
            ->select($key)
            ->lazyById(100);
    }

    private function createDelivery(Outbox $message, WebhookEndpoint $endpoint): WebhookDelivery
    {
        /** @var class-string<WebhookDelivery> $deliveryClass */
        $deliveryClass = Config::webhookDeliveryModel();

        // forceFill (P1.4/F-P1.8-02): WebhookDelivery is intentionally fully
        // guarded — this is the relay's own trusted seam, never raw request input.
        /** @var WebhookDelivery $delivery */
        $delivery = new $deliveryClass;
        $delivery->forceFill([
            'endpoint_id' => $endpoint->getKey(),
            'event' => $message->event,
            'payload' => $message->payload,
            'status' => 'pending',
            'attempts' => 0,
            'next_retry_at' => now(),
        ]);
        $delivery->save();

        return $delivery;
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/core/src/Webhooks/RecordsOutbox.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Webhooks;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Events\Dispatcher;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Throwable;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Events\Access\NodeGrantCreated;
use Vaulter\Events\Access\NodeGrantRevoked;
use Vaulter\Events\Sharing\NodeShareCreated;
use Vaulter\Events\Sharing\NodeShareRevoked;

/**
 * The Laravel event subscriber that stages the KERNEL domain events (Access grants,
 * Sharing links) in the transactional outbox (master plan §2.11 / D14 / M3b).
 * Registered in the core service provider ONLY when the 'reliability' feature is
 * enabled, it routes each kernel event to the OutboxRecorder, which writes ONE
 * v_outbox row (idempotent by event_id).
 *
 * The Drive file/folder/upload events are staged by the twin subscriber in
 * vaulter-drive (P2 #5 / ADR-0015) through the same OutboxRecorder public seam — core
 * no longer references Vaulter\Drive\Events\*, and the outbox contract is unchanged.
 *
 * TRANSACTIONAL-OUTBOX SEMANTICS: the kernel domain events are dispatched INSIDE the
 * mutation transaction (F-P1.7-03), so this subscriber writes the outbox row in the
 * SAME transaction as the mutation — the two commit (or roll back) together. There is
 * no crash window between the mutation's commit and staging, giving true at-least-once;
 * consumers still DEDUPE on event_id.
 *
 * RESILIENCE: every handler is wrapped so a recorder exception is logged and
 * swallowed — staging an event must never break the originating action. Because the
 * handler now runs INSIDE the mutation transaction, the recorder call is wrapped in a
 * SAVEPOINT: on PostgreSQL a failed statement aborts the whole transaction, so merely
 * catching the exception would leave it poisoned and the mutation's COMMIT would fail
 * (silently rolling the grant/share back). The savepoint confines a staging failure to
 * itself, so it truly never rolls the mutation back (review P0.2 / F-P1.7-03).
 */
final readonly class RecordsOutbox
{
    public function __construct(
        private OutboxRecorder $recorder,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Map every fanned-out kernel event to its handler. The dispatcher invokes the
     * named method with the event instance.
     */
    public function subscribe(Dispatcher $events): void
    {
        $events->listen(NodeGrantCreated::class, [self::class, 'handleGrantCreated']);
        $events->listen(NodeGrantRevoked::class, [self::class, 'handleGrantRevoked']);
        $events->listen(NodeShareCreated::class, [self::class, 'handleShareCreated']);
        $events->listen(NodeShareRevoked::class, [self::class, 'handleShareRevoked']);
    }

    public function handleGrantCreated(NodeGrantCreated $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->recordGrantCreated($event), NodeGrantCreated::class);
    }

    public function handleGrantRevoked(NodeGrantRevoked $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->recordGrantRevoked($event), NodeGrantRevoked::class);
    }

    public function handleShareCreated(NodeShareCreated $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->recordShareCreated($event), NodeShareCreated::class);
    }

    public function handleShareRevoked(NodeShareRevoked $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->recordShareRevoked($event), NodeShareRevoked::class);
    }

    /**
     * Run a recorder call, swallowing + logging any failure. Staging must never break
     * the originating action. The handler fires INSIDE the mutation transaction
     * (F-P1.7-03), so the call is wrapped in a SAVEPOINT (a nested VaulterDatabase::transaction): a
     * failed staging statement rolls back to the savepoint — un-poisoning the outer
     * transaction on PostgreSQL — instead of aborting the mutation's COMMIT. A missed
     * outbox row is then a logged degradation, not a mutation rollback.
     *
     * @param callable(): mixed $record
     */
    private function safely(callable $record, string $event): void
    {
        try {
            $this->database->transaction(fn (): mixed => $record());
        } catch (Throwable $exception) {
            Log::warning('Vaulter outbox staging failed; the originating action is unaffected.', [
                'event' => $event,
                'exception' => $exception->getMessage(),
            ]);
        }
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/core/src/Webhooks/WebhookRetryDispatcher.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Webhooks;

use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Bus;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\TraceRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Jobs\Webhooks\DeliverWebhook;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\WebhookDelivery;
use Vaulter\Observability\TracingAttributes;
use Vaulter\Observability\TracingSpanNames;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;

/**
 * The SINGLE owner of webhook-delivery re-dispatch (§2.11 / F-P1.7-05). Both
 * vaulter:relay-outbox and vaulter:retry-webhooks route their due-retry sweep through
 * here, so two ticks (or the two commands overlapping) can never fan the same failed
 * delivery out twice.
 *
 * A due batch is claimed under a row lock inside a transaction and its next_retry_at is
 * re-armed forward by a short claim window BEFORE dispatch — a visibility timeout: a
 * concurrent or immediately-following sweep no longer sees the row as due, and where
 * the row lock degrades (SQLite) the committed re-arm still guarantees a single owner
 * per claim. If the queued job is lost, the window lapses and the row becomes due
 * again (self-healing) — DeliverWebhook overwrites next_retry_at with the real backoff
 * on the next failure, or clears it on success. A new intermediate status was avoided
 * on purpose: it would strand a delivery (neither due nor terminal) if the job were
 * lost mid-flight.
 */
final class WebhookRetryDispatcher
{
    private const int MAX_CLAIM_ROWS = 1000;

    public function __construct(
        private readonly MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private readonly TraceRecorder $tracer,
        private readonly VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Claim at most 1000 due retries and dispatch each delivery job once. Returns
     * how many were re-dispatched; later ticks drain the remaining due rows.
     */
    public function redispatchDue(): int
    {
        return $this->tracer->span(
            name: TracingSpanNames::WEBHOOK_RETRY,
            operation: fn (): int => $this->metrics->timing(
                name: 'vaulter_webhook_retry_tick_seconds',
                operation: function (): int {
                    $deliveries = $this->claimDue();

                    $this->metrics->histogram(
                        name: 'vaulter_webhook_retry_lockset_rows',
                        value: $deliveries->count(),
                    );

                    foreach ($deliveries as $delivery) {
                        Bus::dispatch(new DeliverWebhook(deliveryId: Identity::stringify($delivery->getKey())));
                    }

                    return $deliveries->count();
                },
            ),
            attributes: TracingAttributes::forTarget(targetId: 'webhook.pipeline', layer: 'action'),
        );
    }

    /**
     * Atomically read the due retries under a row lock and re-arm their next_retry_at
     * forward by the claim window, so the same row is claimed once per call.
     *
     * @return Collection<int, WebhookDelivery>
     */
    private function claimDue(): Collection
    {
        /** @var class-string<WebhookDelivery> $deliveryClass */
        $deliveryClass = Config::webhookDeliveryModel();

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($deliveryClass): Collection {
            $query = $deliveryClass::query()->dueForRetry();

            /** @var Collection<int, WebhookDelivery> $deliveries */
            $deliveries = $query
                ->orderBy('next_retry_at')
                ->orderBy($query->getModel()->getQualifiedKeyName())
                ->limit(self::MAX_CLAIM_ROWS)
                ->lockForUpdate()
                ->get();

            $claimUntil = now()->addSeconds(Config::webhookRetryClaimSeconds());

            $deliveries->each(static function (WebhookDelivery $delivery) use ($claimUntil): void {
                $delivery->forceFill(['next_retry_at' => $claimUntil])->save();
            });

            return $deliveries;
        });
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/core/tests/Arch/PublicApiSnapshotTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Contracts\ProblemException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNotFoundException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\StorageNotFoundException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\VaulterException;
use Vaulter\Facades\Vaulter;

/**
 * VLT-REL P2.2 — executable freeze of the public composer surface for the `0.1.0`
 * tag. The human-readable SSOT is `plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/public-api-surface.md`
 * (P2.1); THIS test is its enforceable counterpart: it reflects the LIVE signatures of
 * every `Vaulter\Contracts\**` interface plus the `Vaulter\Facades\Vaulter` facade,
 * canonicalises them into a stable manifest, and compares against a committed golden.
 * Any unplanned change to the surface (a removed/renamed method, a narrowed parameter
 * type, a new contract, a dropped facade `@method`) reddens the build.
 *
 * WHY reflection, not the doc: the doc can drift silently; this test can't — the golden
 * is generated from real signatures, so a `public-api.md:19` breaking change (removal /
 * rename / type narrowing) is caught mechanically, not by review vigilance.
 *
 * Canonicalisation (D5 — signatures only; config/resources/bindings live in the doc):
 *   • Contracts are DISCOVERED by scanning `src/Contracts/**` — so a NEW contract is
 *     frozen automatically (its absence from the golden reddens the build), not just the
 *     ones enumerated today.
 *   • Full signature: parameter name + type (union/intersection/nullable normalised),
 *     by-ref, variadic, default value; return type. Named-argument discipline
 *     (`php:named-arguments`) makes parameter NAMES part of the contract, so they are
 *     frozen too.
 *   • `@internal`-marked interfaces/methods are EXCLUDED (outside the BC promise,
 *     `public-api.md:22`).
 *   • Deterministic order: contracts sorted by FQCN, methods sorted by name, union/
 *     intersection members sorted — so a harmless reorder never reddens the build while
 *     a real signature diff reads cleanly.
 *   • The facade is frozen by its `@method` docblock (it stands over the resolved
 *     `VaulterManager`, Laravel Manager idiom — SSOT §2), not by reflecting the thin
 *     Facade class (whose only real method is the protected accessor).
 *
 * AMEND (adding surface legally after the tag): regenerate the golden with
 * `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT=1`, add a new `D#`, sync `plan.md` §7 Contracts, and ship a CHANGELOG
 * MINOR — see `plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/AMEND.md`. Regenerating the golden OUTSIDE
 * that procedure (no `D#`) is a `[POSSIBLE-DEFECT]` per `.claude/rules/tests.md`.
 * `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT` is env-gated and is NEVER set by any CI workflow.
 */
if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotType')) {
    /**
     * Normalise a reflection type to a canonical, deterministic string. Union and
     * intersection members are sorted so a source-level reorder (not a BC change) does
     * not redden the build. Non-builtin types carry a leading `\` for an unambiguous FQCN.
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotType(?ReflectionType $type): string
    {
        if ($type === null) {
            return '';
        }

        if ($type instanceof ReflectionUnionType) {
            $parts = array_map('vaulterSnapshotType', $type->getTypes());
            sort($parts);

            return implode('|', $parts);
        }

        if ($type instanceof ReflectionIntersectionType) {
            $parts = array_map('vaulterSnapshotType', $type->getTypes());
            sort($parts);

            return implode('&', $parts);
        }

        /** @var ReflectionNamedType $type */
        $name = $type->getName();
        $lower = strtolower($name);
        $prefix = ($type->allowsNull() && $lower !== 'null' && $lower !== 'mixed') ? '?' : '';

        return $prefix.($type->isBuiltin() ? '' : '\\').$name;
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotValue')) {
    /**
     * Compact, deterministic rendering of a parameter default value (scalars, null,
     * shallow arrays — the only shapes contract defaults take).
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotValue(mixed $value): string
    {
        if ($value === null) {
            return 'null';
        }

        if (is_bool($value)) {
            return $value ? 'true' : 'false';
        }

        if (is_string($value)) {
            return "'".$value."'";
        }

        if (is_array($value)) {
            return '['.implode(', ', array_map('vaulterSnapshotValue', $value)).']';
        }

        if (is_int($value) || is_float($value)) {
            return (string) $value;
        }

        return get_debug_type($value);
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotMethod')) {
    /** Serialise one public method to `name(type $p = default, …): return`. */
    function vaulterSnapshotMethod(ReflectionMethod $method): string
    {
        $params = [];

        foreach ($method->getParameters() as $param) {
            $piece = vaulterSnapshotType($param->getType());
            $piece .= ' ';
            $piece .= $param->isPassedByReference() ? '&' : '';
            $piece .= $param->isVariadic() ? '...' : '';
            $piece .= '$'.$param->getName();

            if ($param->isDefaultValueAvailable()) {
                $piece .= ' = '.($param->isDefaultValueConstant()
                    ? (string) $param->getDefaultValueConstantName()
                    : vaulterSnapshotValue($param->getDefaultValue()));
            }

            $params[] = trim($piece);
        }

        $return = vaulterSnapshotType($method->getReturnType());

        return $method->getName().'('.implode(', ', $params).')'.($return !== '' ? ': '.$return : '');
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotInterface')) {
    /**
     * Serialise one contract: FQCN header + its public, non-`@internal` methods (sorted).
     *
     * @param class-string $fqcn
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotInterface(string $fqcn): string
    {
        $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
        $lines = [];

        foreach ($reflection->getMethods(ReflectionMethod::IS_PUBLIC) as $method) {
            $doc = $method->getDocComment();

            if ($doc !== false && str_contains($doc, '@internal')) {
                continue;
            }

            $lines[] = '  '.vaulterSnapshotMethod($method);
        }

        sort($lines);

        return $fqcn."\n".implode("\n", $lines);
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotContracts')) {
    /**
     * Discover every `*\Contracts\**` interface under a package src tree and map each
     * file to its FQCN (PSR-4: basename === declared type). `@internal`-marked types are
     * dropped from the frozen set.
     *
     * @return list<class-string> sorted FQCNs
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotContracts(string $contractsDir, string $namespaceRoot): array
    {
        if (!is_dir($contractsDir)) {
            return [];
        }

        /** @var Iterator<SplFileInfo> $files */
        $files = new RegexIterator(
            new RecursiveIteratorIterator(new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($contractsDir, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS)),
            '/\.php$/',
        );

        $fqcns = [];

        foreach ($files as $file) {
            $relative = substr(str_replace($contractsDir.'/', '', $file->getPathname()), 0, -4);
            $fqcn = $namespaceRoot.'\\'.str_replace('/', '\\', $relative);

            // Per-branch interface_exists()/class_exists() narrow $fqcn to class-string
            // (an OR of the two does not), so ReflectionClass type-checks without a
            // suppression. Contracts are interfaces by invariant (arch 'contracts are
            // interfaces'); the class branch keeps discovery honest if that ever slips.
            if (interface_exists($fqcn)) {
                $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
            } elseif (class_exists($fqcn)) {
                $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
            } else {
                continue;
            }

            $doc = $reflection->getDocComment();

            if ($doc !== false && str_contains($doc, '@internal')) {
                continue;
            }

            $fqcns[] = $fqcn;
        }

        sort($fqcns);

        return array_values(array_unique($fqcns));
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotFacade')) {
    /** Freeze the facade by its `@method` docblock (surface over the resolved manager). */
    function vaulterSnapshotFacade(string $facadeFqcn): string
    {
        $doc = (new ReflectionClass($facadeFqcn))->getDocComment() ?: '';
        preg_match_all('/@method\s+(.+)$/m', $doc, $matches);

        $methods = array_map(
            static fn (string $line): string => trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $line)),
            $matches[1],
        );

        sort($methods);

        return 'FACADE '.$facadeFqcn."\n".implode("\n", array_map(
            static fn (string $m): string => '  '.$m,
            $methods,
        ));
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotAssert')) {
    /**
     * Shared gate body: compare the freshly reflected manifest against the committed
     * golden, or regenerate it under the env-gated `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT` escape hatch.
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotAssert(string $manifest, string $goldenPath): void
    {
        if (getenv('UPDATE_SNAPSHOT') === '1') {
            @mkdir(dirname($goldenPath), 0o755, true);
            file_put_contents($goldenPath, $manifest);
            expect(is_file($goldenPath))->toBeTrue();

            return;
        }

        expect(is_file($goldenPath))->toBeTrue();

        expect($manifest)->toBe(
            file_get_contents($goldenPath),
            'Public API surface changed. If this is INTENTIONAL and ADDITIVE, follow AMEND '
            .'(plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/AMEND.md): regenerate the golden with UPDATE_SNAPSHOT=1, '
            .'add a D#, sync plan.md §7 Contracts, ship a CHANGELOG MINOR. If it is BREAKING '
            .'(removed/renamed method, narrowed type), that is a SemVer decision — escalate to the owner.',
        );
    }
}

test('vaulter-core public API surface matches the frozen golden (VLT-REL P2.2)', function (): void {
    $contracts = vaulterSnapshotContracts(__DIR__.'/../../src/Contracts', 'Vaulter\Contracts');

    // Discovery must find the enumerated surface — a scan that silently finds nothing
    // would make the whole freeze an inert no-op.
    expect($contracts)->not->toBe([]);

    $blocks = array_map('vaulterSnapshotInterface', $contracts);
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(VaulterDatabase::class);
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface(ProblemException::class);
    foreach ([NodeNotFoundException::class => 'forId', StorageNotFoundException::class => 'forId', VaulterException::class => 'render'] as $class => $method) {
        $blocks[] = $class."\n  ".vaulterSnapshotMethod(new ReflectionMethod($class, $method));
    }
    $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotFacade(Vaulter::class);

    foreach (vaulterSnapshotContracts(__DIR__.'/../../src/Events', 'Vaulter\Events') as $eventClass) {
        $reflection = new ReflectionClass($eventClass);
        $properties = [];
        foreach ($reflection->getProperties(ReflectionProperty::IS_PUBLIC) as $property) {
            $properties[] = '  '.($property->isReadOnly() ? 'readonly ' : '')
                .vaulterSnapshotType($property->getType()).' $'.$property->getName();
        }
        sort($properties);
        $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface($eventClass)."\n".implode("\n", $properties);
    }

    vaulterSnapshotAssert(implode("\n\n", $blocks)."\n", __DIR__.'/__snapshots__/public-api.txt');
});

END FILE

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Console\Events\CommandFinished;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Queue\Job;
use Illuminate\Queue\Events\JobFailed;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Bus;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Queue;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Input\ArrayInput;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Output\BufferedOutput;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Webhooks\OutboundUrlGuard;
use Vaulter\Enums\Webhooks\WebhookEvent;
use Vaulter\Jobs\Webhooks\DeliverWebhook;
use Vaulter\Load\Listeners\RecordCommandPeakMemory;
use Vaulter\Load\Listeners\RecordFailedJob;
use Vaulter\Load\Observability\LoadStateMetrics;
use Vaulter\Testing\FakeMetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Tests\Webhooks\WebhookTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\OutboxRecorder;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\OutboxRelay;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\WebhookRetryDispatcher;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\WebhookSigner;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.observability.enabled', true);
    config()->set('vaulter.observability.recorder', FakeMetricsRecorder::class);
    $this->app->forgetInstance(MetricsRecorder::class);
});

it('records vaulter_outbox_backlog from pending outbox rows', function (): void {
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox(['status' => 'pending']);
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox(['status' => 'dispatched']);

    app(LoadStateMetrics::class)->observeOutboxBacklog();

    app(MetricsRecorder::class)->assertGauge(name: 'vaulter_outbox_backlog');
});

it('records vaulter_outbox_stage_duration_seconds when staging a new row', function (): void {
    app(OutboxRecorder::class)->stage(
        event: WebhookEvent::GrantCreated,
        eventId: (string) Str::ulid(),
        panel: 'default',
        topic: 'node-1',
        payload: ['grant_id' => 'g-1'],
        actorId: 1,
    );

    app(MetricsRecorder::class)->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_outbox_stage_duration_seconds');
});

it('records outbox relay metrics for a pending row', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();
    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['events' => ['grant.created'], 'url' => 'https://hooks.test/other']);
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox();

    app(OutboxRelay::class)->process();

    $recorder = app(MetricsRecorder::class);
    $recorder->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_outbox_relay_duration_seconds');
    $recorder->assertCounter(name: 'vaulter_outbox_relayed_total');
    $recorder->assertCounter(name: 'vaulter_outbox_endpoints_scanned_total');

    $scanned = collect($recorder->emissions())
        ->where('name', 'vaulter_outbox_endpoints_scanned_total')
        ->sum('value');

    expect($scanned)->toBe(1);
});

it('records webhook retry tick and lockset metrics', function (): void {
    Bus::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::delivery(
        WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(),
        ['status' => 'failed', 'next_retry_at' => now()->subSecond()],
    );

    app(WebhookRetryDispatcher::class)->redispatchDue();

    $recorder = app(MetricsRecorder::class);
    $recorder->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_webhook_retry_tick_seconds');
    $recorder->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_webhook_retry_lockset_rows');
});

it('records webhook delivery metrics on successful deliver', function (): void {
    Http::fake(['https://hooks.test/*' => Http::response('', 200)]);

    $delivery = WebhookTestHelpers::delivery(WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint());

    (new DeliverWebhook(deliveryId: (string) $delivery->getKey()))
        ->handle(app(WebhookSigner::class), app(OutboundUrlGuard::class));

    $recorder = app(MetricsRecorder::class);
    $recorder->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_webhook_queue_wait_seconds', labels: ['outcome' => 'success']);
    $recorder->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_webhook_delivery_duration_seconds', labels: ['outcome' => 'success']);
    $recorder->assertCounter(name: 'vaulter_webhook_delivered_total', labels: ['outcome' => 'success']);
});

it('records vaulter_failed_jobs_total on job failed event', function (): void {
    $exception = new RuntimeException('job failed hard');
    $job = Mockery::mock(Job::class);

    app(RecordFailedJob::class)->handle(new JobFailed(connectionName: 'redis', job: $job, exception: $exception));

    app(MetricsRecorder::class)->assertCounter(name: 'vaulter_failed_jobs_total', labels: ['outcome' => 'failure']);
});

it('records vaulter_command_peak_memory_bytes for vaulter artisan commands', function (): void {
    $commands = [
        'vaulter:sweep-uploads',
        'vaulter:gc-blobs',
        'vaulter:reconcile-blobs',
        'vaulter:purge-trash',
        'vaulter:prune-webhooks',
        'vaulter:retry-webhooks',
        'vaulter:relay-outbox',
    ];

    foreach ($commands as $command) {
        app(RecordCommandPeakMemory::class)->handle(new CommandFinished(
            command: $command,
            input: new ArrayInput([]),
            output: new BufferedOutput,
            exitCode: 0,
        ));

        app(MetricsRecorder::class)->assertGauge(
            name: 'vaulter_command_peak_memory_bytes',
            labels: ['command' => $command],
        );
    }
});

it('records prune webhook metrics when rows are reclaimed', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.features.reliability', true);
    config()->set('vaulter.webhooks.retention_days', 0);

    $threshold = now()->subDay();
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox([
        'status' => 'dispatched',
        'dispatched_at' => $threshold,
    ]);

    Artisan::call('vaulter:prune-webhooks');

    $recorder = app(MetricsRecorder::class);
    $recorder->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_prune_duration_seconds');
    $recorder->assertCounter(name: 'vaulter_prune_rows_total');
});

it('exposes vaulter_pg_lock_wait_seconds from the harness collector script', function (): void {
    $script = dirname(__DIR__, 5).'/load/scripts/collect-postgres-lock-waits.sh';

    expect(is_file($script))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(file_get_contents($script))->toContain('vaulter_pg_lock_wait_seconds');
});

FILE: packages/core/tests/TestCase.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Tests;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Orchestra\Testbench\TestCase as OrchestraTestCase;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Webhooks\HostResolver;
use Vaulter\Drive\VaulterDriveServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\Media\VaulterMediaServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\Tests\Webhooks\FakeHostResolver;
use Vaulter\VaulterServiceProvider;

abstract class TestCase extends OrchestraTestCase
{
    #[\Override]
    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();

        // No test hits real DNS or a socket (§2.11 / F-P1.7-01): the outbound SSRF
        // guard resolves through a fake resolver that maps hosts to a public address
        // by default, so a delivery/registration to a test host (hooks.test) passes
        // the range check. An SSRF test overrides the map via
        // app(HostResolver::class)->set('evil.test', ['127.0.0.1']).
        $this->app->singleton(HostResolver::class, static fn (): FakeHostResolver => new FakeHostResolver);
    }

    /**
     * @param  Application              $app
     * @return array<int, class-string>
     */
    protected function getPackageProviders($app): array
    {
        return [
            VaulterServiceProvider::class,
            // The CAS engine (ContentStore/StorageDriver/BlobStore) lives in the
            // vaulter-media package (P2 #5); the core Drive upload/download/copy
            // suites resolve those bindings through its provider.
            VaulterMediaServiceProvider::class,
            // Drive behaviour (StorageManager, DriveHandle factory, resumable
            // uploads, PurgeTrash/SweepUploads, drive routes, Drive-event
            // Activity/outbox subscribers) lives in vaulter-drive (P2 #5, D1). The
            // cross-cutting core suites (Activity/Webhooks feeds fed by Drive events,
            // the full-stack Feature specs) resolve it through this provider.
            VaulterDriveServiceProvider::class,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param Application $app
     */
    protected function defineEnvironment($app): void
    {
        $app['config']->set('database.default', 'testing');
        $app['config']->set('database.connections.testing', $this->suiteDatabaseConnection());

        // The package's `vaulter-public`/`vaulter-uploads` named rate limiters
        // (P1.3 / F-P1.8-01) are backed by the Laravel cache. Testbench's own
        // default cache store is 'database', which needs a `cache` table this
        // suite's migrations never create — pin the in-memory 'array' store so a
        // throttled route never 500s on a missing table.
        $app['config']->set('cache.default', 'array');

        // Drop the route middleware group so tests do not depend on the host's
        // 'api' group definition; authorization is exercised via the resolver.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.middleware', []);

        // The base suite runs OPEN: the env-gated NullRoleDriver (non-prod only,
        // H.2 / D6″) grants every actor full access, so the M1c Drive tests keep
        // their open behaviour. The M2a ACL tests opt INTO the 'database' driver
        // explicitly to exercise real grant resolution.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.drivers.permission', 'null');

        // Pin the auto-increment strategy for the base suite. The published
        // config now defaults to ulid (D9); the legacy default-panel document
        // tests assert bigint keys, so the foundation suite keeps bigint and the
        // UlidTestCase exercises the ulid path explicitly.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.ids.default', 'bigint');
    }

    protected function defineDatabaseMigrations(): void
    {
        $this->loadMigrationsFrom(__DIR__.'/../database/migrations');
    }

    /**
     * The connection the whole suite runs on. Default: in-memory SQLite — the fast,
     * dependency-free lane the entire suite uses locally and in the main CI matrix.
     *
     * Opt INTO Postgres by exporting DB_CONNECTION=pgsql (plus the DB_* coordinates):
     * the dedicated pgsql CI job does this to exercise the real advisory-xact-lock /
     * FOR UPDATE critical sections (race-free blob commit, destructive orphan prune)
     * that SQLite cannot model — every such test SKIPS unless the live driver is pgsql
     * (master plan P4 #11). No env, no Postgres needed: the suite stays on SQLite.
     *
     * @return array<string, string|bool|null>
     */
    protected function suiteDatabaseConnection(): array
    {
        if (env('DB_CONNECTION') !== 'pgsql') {
            return [
                'driver' => 'sqlite',
                'database' => ':memory:',
                'prefix' => '',
            ];
        }

        return [
            'driver' => 'pgsql',
            'host' => (string) env('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1'),
            'port' => (string) env('DB_PORT', '5432'),
            'database' => (string) env('DB_DATABASE', 'vaulter_test'),
            'username' => (string) env('DB_USERNAME', 'vaulter'),
            'password' => (string) env('DB_PASSWORD', ''),
            'charset' => 'utf8',
            'prefix' => '',
            'prefix_indexes' => true,
            'search_path' => 'public',
            'sslmode' => 'prefer',
        ];
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/core/tests/Webhooks/OutboxRelayTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Bus;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Queue;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Webhooks\OutboundUrlGuard;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeCreated;
use Vaulter\Enums\Webhooks\WebhookEvent;
use Vaulter\Jobs\Webhooks\DeliverWebhook;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\Outbox;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\WebhookDelivery;
use Vaulter\Tests\Access\AccessTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Tests\Webhooks\WebhookTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\OutboxRecorder;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\OutboxRelay;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\WebhookSigner;

/**
 * The transactional-outbox RELAY (§2.11 / D14 / M3b), ported from chatom: process()
 * fans pending outbox rows out into per-endpoint WebhookDelivery rows + DeliverWebhook
 * jobs; vaulter:relay-outbox ticks and re-arms due retries. Idempotency is row-state
 * driven (pending → dispatched), so overlapping ticks never double-deliver.
 *
 * Jobs are faked (Queue/Bus::fake) so dispatch is asserted without the HTTP job.
 */
function relay(): OutboxRelay
{
    return app(OutboxRelay::class);
}

it('sends the same occurrence id on the original delivery and a relay retry', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();
    Bus::fake();
    Http::fake(['https://hooks.test/*' => Http::sequence()->push('', 500)->push('', 200)]);

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();
    $storage = AccessTestHelpers::storage();
    $node = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, 'Retry');
    Outbox::query()->delete();
    $event = new NodeCreated(node: $node, actorId: 7);
    $message = app(OutboxRecorder::class)->stage(
        event: WebhookEvent::NodeCreated,
        eventId: $event->eventId,
        panel: $node->panel,
        topic: (string) $node->getKey(),
        payload: ['node_id' => (string) $node->getKey()],
        actorId: 7,
    );

    expect(relay()->process())->toBe(1);
    $delivery = WebhookDelivery::query()->sole();
    $job = new DeliverWebhook(deliveryId: (string) $delivery->getKey());
    $job->handle(app(WebhookSigner::class), app(OutboundUrlGuard::class));
    $delivery->refresh();
    expect($delivery->status)->toBe('failed');

    $delivery->forceFill(['next_retry_at' => now()->subSecond()])->save();
    $this->artisan('vaulter:relay-outbox')->assertSuccessful();
    Bus::assertDispatched(DeliverWebhook::class, function (DeliverWebhook $retry) use ($delivery): bool {
        return (new ReflectionProperty($retry, 'deliveryId'))->getValue($retry) === (string) $delivery->getKey();
    });
    $job->handle(app(WebhookSigner::class), app(OutboundUrlGuard::class));

    expect($delivery->refresh()->status)->toBe('delivered')
        ->and($delivery->attempts)->toBe(2)
        ->and($message->refresh()->event_id)->toBe($event->eventId)
        ->and(WebhookDelivery::query()->count())->toBe(1);
    Http::assertSentCount(2);
    foreach (Http::recorded() as [$request]) {
        expect($request->data()['event_id'])->toBe($event->eventId);
    }
});

// ─────────────────────── process() — fan-out ───────────────────────

it('fans a pending outbox row out to a subscribed active endpoint and dispatches its job', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    $endpoint = WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();
    $message = WebhookTestHelpers::outbox();

    $processed = relay()->process();

    expect($processed)->toBe(1);

    $deliveries = WebhookDelivery::query()->get();

    expect($deliveries)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and((string) $deliveries->first()->endpoint_id)->toBe((string) $endpoint->getKey())
        ->and($deliveries->first()->event)->toBe('node.created')
        ->and($deliveries->first()->status)->toBe('pending')
        ->and($deliveries->first()->attempts)->toBe(0)
        ->and($deliveries->first()->payload['node_id'])->toBe('node-1');

    $message->refresh();

    expect($message->status)->toBe('dispatched')
        ->and($message->dispatched_at)->toBeInstanceOf(Carbon::class);

    Queue::assertPushed(DeliverWebhook::class, 1);
});

it('is idempotent: a second process() does not re-fan an already-dispatched row', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox();

    $first = relay()->process();
    $second = relay()->process();

    expect($first)->toBe(1)
        ->and($second)->toBe(0)
        ->and(WebhookDelivery::query()->count())->toBe(1);

    Queue::assertPushed(DeliverWebhook::class, 1);
});

it('does not double fan out when a second relay tick starts after the first claim', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox();

    $overlapResult = null;
    DB::listen(static function ($query) use (&$overlapResult): void {
        if ($overlapResult === null && str_contains($query->sql, 'webhook_endpoints')) {
            $overlapResult = relay()->process();
        }
    });

    expect(relay()->process())->toBe(1)
        ->and($overlapResult)->toBe(0)
        ->and(WebhookDelivery::query()->count())->toBe(1);

    Queue::assertPushed(DeliverWebhook::class, 1);
});

it('does not create a delivery for an endpoint not subscribed to the event', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['events' => ['share.created']]);
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox(['event' => 'node.created']);

    $processed = relay()->process();

    expect($processed)->toBe(1)
        ->and(WebhookDelivery::query()->count())->toBe(0);

    Queue::assertNothingPushed();
});

it('does not create a delivery for an inactive endpoint', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['is_active' => false]);
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox();

    $processed = relay()->process();

    expect($processed)->toBe(1)
        ->and(WebhookDelivery::query()->count())->toBe(0);

    Queue::assertNothingPushed();
});

it('fans out to every subscribed active endpoint and skips the unsubscribed/inactive', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    $a = WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['url' => 'https://a.test/hook']);
    $b = WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['url' => 'https://b.test/hook', 'events' => ['node.created', 'share.created']]);
    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['url' => 'https://c.test/hook', 'events' => ['share.created']]); // unsubscribed
    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['url' => 'https://d.test/hook', 'is_active' => false]);          // inactive

    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox(['event' => 'node.created']);

    relay()->process();

    $endpointIds = WebhookDelivery::query()->pluck('endpoint_id')->map(fn ($id): string => (string) $id);

    expect(WebhookDelivery::query()->count())->toBe(2)
        ->and($endpointIds)->toContain((string) $a->getKey())
        ->and($endpointIds)->toContain((string) $b->getKey());

    Queue::assertPushed(DeliverWebhook::class, 2);
});

it('filters subscriptions in SQL and fans out in bounded key-ordered pages', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    $matchingIds = [];

    for ($index = 0; $index < 205; $index++) {
        $matchingIds[] = WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint()->getKey();
    }

    for ($index = 0; $index < 120; $index++) {
        WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['events' => ['share.created']]);
    }

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint(['is_active' => false]);
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox();

    $endpointQueries = [];
    DB::listen(static function ($query) use (&$endpointQueries): void {
        if (str_contains($query->sql, 'webhook_endpoints')) {
            $endpointQueries[] = $query->sql;
        }
    });

    expect(relay()->process())->toBe(1);

    $actualIds = WebhookDelivery::query()->orderBy('id')->pluck('endpoint_id')->all();
    expect($actualIds)->toBe($matchingIds)
        ->and(count($endpointQueries))->toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);

    foreach ($endpointQueries as $sql) {
        expect($sql)->toContain('events');
    }

    Queue::assertPushed(DeliverWebhook::class, 205);
});

it('leaves a not-yet-available outbox row pending', function (): void {
    Queue::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox(['available_at' => now()->addHour()]);

    expect(relay()->process())->toBe(0)
        ->and(Outbox::query()->where('status', 'pending')->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(WebhookDelivery::query()->count())->toBe(0);

    Queue::assertNothingPushed();
});

// ─────────────────────── vaulter:relay-outbox ───────────────────────

it('vaulter:relay-outbox drains a pending outbox row into a delivery and a job', function (): void {
    Bus::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();
    $message = WebhookTestHelpers::outbox();

    $this->artisan('vaulter:relay-outbox')->assertSuccessful();

    expect($message->refresh()->status)->toBe('dispatched')
        ->and(WebhookDelivery::query()->where('status', 'pending')->count())->toBe(1);

    Bus::assertDispatched(DeliverWebhook::class, 1);
});

it('vaulter:relay-outbox re-dispatches a due failed retry but skips a not-yet-due one', function (): void {
    Bus::fake();

    $endpoint = WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();

    $due = WebhookTestHelpers::delivery($endpoint, [
        'status' => 'failed',
        'attempts' => 1,
        'next_retry_at' => now()->subMinute(),
    ]);

    WebhookTestHelpers::delivery($endpoint, [
        'status' => 'failed',
        'attempts' => 1,
        'next_retry_at' => now()->addHour(),
    ]);

    $this->artisan('vaulter:relay-outbox')->assertSuccessful();

    Bus::assertDispatched(DeliverWebhook::class, function (DeliverWebhook $job) use ($due): bool {
        return (new ReflectionProperty($job, 'deliveryId'))->getValue($job) === (string) $due->getKey();
    });
    Bus::assertDispatched(DeliverWebhook::class, 1);
});

it('vaulter:relay-outbox does not re-dispatch terminal deliveries', function (): void {
    Bus::fake();

    $endpoint = WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();

    WebhookTestHelpers::delivery($endpoint, ['status' => 'delivered', 'attempts' => 1, 'next_retry_at' => now()->subMinute()]);
    WebhookTestHelpers::delivery($endpoint, ['status' => 'dead', 'attempts' => 5, 'next_retry_at' => now()->subMinute()]);

    $this->artisan('vaulter:relay-outbox')->assertSuccessful();

    Bus::assertNotDispatched(DeliverWebhook::class);
});

it('vaulter:relay-outbox honours the --limit option', function (): void {
    Bus::fake();

    WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox(['event_id' => 'a', 'available_at' => now()->subSeconds(2)]);
    WebhookTestHelpers::outbox(['event_id' => 'b', 'available_at' => now()->subSecond()]);

    $this->artisan('vaulter:relay-outbox', ['--limit' => 1])->assertSuccessful();

    expect(Outbox::query()->where('status', 'pending')->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(Outbox::query()->where('status', 'dispatched')->count())->toBe(1);

    Bus::assertDispatched(DeliverWebhook::class, 1);
});

// ─────────────────────── vaulter:retry-webhooks ───────────────────────

it('vaulter:retry-webhooks re-queues only due failed deliveries', function (): void {
    Bus::fake();

    $endpoint = WebhookTestHelpers::endpoint();

    $due = WebhookTestHelpers::delivery($endpoint, ['status' => 'failed', 'attempts' => 1, 'next_retry_at' => now()->subMinute()]);
    WebhookTestHelpers::delivery($endpoint, ['status' => 'failed', 'attempts' => 1, 'next_retry_at' => now()->addHour()]);
    WebhookTestHelpers::delivery($endpoint, ['status' => 'dead', 'attempts' => 5, 'next_retry_at' => now()->subMinute()]);

    $this->artisan('vaulter:retry-webhooks')->assertSuccessful();

    Bus::assertDispatched(DeliverWebhook::class, function (DeliverWebhook $job) use ($due): bool {
        return (new ReflectionProperty($job, 'deliveryId'))->getValue($job) === (string) $due->getKey();
    });
    Bus::assertDispatched(DeliverWebhook::class, 1);
});
