FILE: packages/core/tests/Webhooks/OutboxStagingTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Vaulter\Access\ValueObjects\Subject;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeCreated;
use Vaulter\Enums\Access\GrantLevel;
use Vaulter\Enums\Webhooks\WebhookEvent;
use Vaulter\Events\Access\NodeGrantCreated;
use Vaulter\Events\Access\NodeGrantRevoked;
use Vaulter\Models\Access\NodeGrant;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\Outbox;
use Vaulter\Tests\Access\AccessTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Tests\Fixtures\TestUser;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\OutboxRecorder;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\RecordsOutbox;

/**
 * The SYNCHRONOUS half of the transactional outbox (§2.11 / D14 / M3b): each fanned-out
 * Drive domain event stages EXACTLY ONE v_outbox row, idempotent by event_id, with a
 * slim payload (no secrets/blob bytes). Staging is gated on the 'reliability' feature
 * and is resilient — a recorder failure never breaks the originating action.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    // The base suite is open (null driver), so the Drive ops succeed and fire events.
    $this->actingAs((new TestUser)->forceFill(['id' => 7]));
});

// ─────────────────────────── one event ⇒ one outbox row ───────────────────────────

it('stages exactly one outbox row when a storage is provisioned', function (): void {
    DriveTestHelpers::drive()->storage();

    $rows = Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::StorageCreated->value)->get();

    expect($rows)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($rows->first()->status)->toBe('pending')
        ->and($rows->first()->payload['name'])->not->toBeNull();
});

it('stages a node.created row carrying the actor and a slim payload', function (): void {
    $storage = AccessTestHelpers::storage();

    // Clear the provisioning rows so we assert the one we cause below.
    Outbox::query()->delete();

    $folder = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, 'reports');
    event(new NodeCreated(node: $folder, actorId: 7));

    $rows = Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::NodeCreated->value)->get();

    expect($rows)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($rows->first()->payload['node_id'])->toEqual($folder->getKey())
        ->and($rows->first()->payload['name'])->toBe('reports')
        ->and($rows->first()->payload['actor_id'])->toEqual(7)
        // The event_id is mirrored into the payload so consumers dedupe on it.
        ->and($rows->first()->payload['event_id'])->toBe($rows->first()->event_id);
});

// ─────────────────────────── idempotency (event_id) ───────────────────────────

it('is idempotent by event_id: re-staging the same occurrence stages no duplicate', function (): void {
    $storage = AccessTestHelpers::storage();
    Outbox::query()->delete();

    $folder = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, 'dup');

    // Two stagings under the SAME event_id key → the second is a no-op → one row. This
    // exercises the core OutboxRecorder::stage() idempotency seam directly (the seam the
    // vaulter-drive subscriber maps its node events through).
    $recorder = app(OutboxRecorder::class);
    $eventId = (string) Str::ulid();

    $stage = fn (): ?Outbox => $recorder->stage(
        event: WebhookEvent::NodeCreated,
        eventId: $eventId,
        panel: $folder->panel,
        topic: (string) $folder->getKey(),
        payload: ['node_id' => $folder->getKey(), 'name' => 'dup'],
        actorId: 7,
    );

    $first = $stage();
    $second = $stage();

    expect($first)->toBeInstanceOf(Outbox::class)
        // The second staging is a no-op (returns null) — idempotent by event_id.
        ->and($second)->toBeNull()
        ->and(Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::NodeCreated->value)->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('keeps distinct grant occurrences with identical parameters and deduplicates their replay', function (): void {
    $storage = AccessTestHelpers::storage();
    $node = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, 'Granted');
    $grant = AccessTestHelpers::grant(node: $node, level: GrantLevel::Reader);
    $subject = new Subject(type: $grant->subject_type, id: $grant->subject_id);
    Outbox::query()->delete();

    foreach ([
        new NodeGrantCreated(grant: $grant, actorId: 7),
        new NodeGrantCreated(grant: $grant, actorId: 7),
        new NodeGrantRevoked(node: $node, subject: $subject, revoked: 1, actorId: 7),
        new NodeGrantRevoked(node: $node, subject: $subject, revoked: 1, actorId: 7),
    ] as $event) {
        event($event);
        event($event);

        $row = Outbox::query()->where('event_id', $event->eventId)->sole();
        expect(Str::isUlid($row->event_id))->toBeTrue()
            ->and($row->payload['event_id'])->toBe($event->eventId);
    }

    expect(Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::GrantCreated->value)->count())->toBe(2)
        ->and(Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::GrantRevoked->value)->count())->toBe(2)
        ->and(Outbox::query()->distinct()->count('event_id'))->toBe(4);
});

it('retains an explicit occurrence id across reconstructed events', function (): void {
    $storage = AccessTestHelpers::storage();
    $node = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, 'Replay');
    Outbox::query()->delete();
    $eventId = (string) Str::ulid();

    event(new NodeCreated(node: $node, actorId: 7, eventId: $eventId));
    event(new NodeCreated(node: $node, actorId: 7, eventId: $eventId));

    $row = Outbox::query()->sole();
    expect($row->event_id)->toBe($eventId)
        ->and($row->payload['event_id'])->toBe($eventId);
});

it('never serializes a share secret — only the non-secret token_prefix travels', function (): void {
    // This is a payload-shape guard: the recorder must not leak token_hash/secret.
    $storage = AccessTestHelpers::storage();
    Outbox::query()->delete();

    // Drive a node and assert the staged payload has no secret-shaped keys anywhere.
    $folder = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, 'safe');
    event(new NodeCreated(node: $folder, actorId: 7));

    $payload = Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::NodeCreated->value)->firstOrFail()->payload;

    expect(array_keys($payload))->not->toContain('signing_secret')
        ->and(array_keys($payload))->not->toContain('token_hash')
        ->and(array_keys($payload))->not->toContain('password');
});

// ─────────────────────────── resilience ───────────────────────────

it('swallows + logs a staging failure so the originating action is unaffected', function (): void {
    Log::spy();

    // A recorder whose recording blows up; the subscriber must swallow + log it. The
    // core RecordsOutbox handles the kernel (grant/share) events; the grant is never
    // inspected because the mocked recorder throws before touching it.
    $recorder = Mockery::mock(OutboxRecorder::class);
    $recorder->shouldReceive('recordGrantCreated')->andThrow(new RuntimeException('boom'));

    $subscriber = new RecordsOutbox($recorder, app(VaulterDatabase::class));

    // Must NOT throw — the failure is logged and swallowed.
    $subscriber->handleGrantCreated(new NodeGrantCreated(grant: new NodeGrant, actorId: 7));

    Log::shouldHaveReceived('warning')->once();
});

END FILE

FILE: packages/core/tests/Webhooks/WebhookTestHelpers.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Tests\Webhooks;

use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\Outbox;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\WebhookDelivery;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\WebhookEndpoint;

/**
 * Row builders for the M3b reliability tests (§2.11): stage outbox rows, register
 * endpoints, and seed deliveries directly so the tests focus on the relay / delivery
 * algorithm, not on row plumbing.
 */
final class WebhookTestHelpers
{
    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $attributes
     */
    public static function outbox(array $attributes = []): Outbox
    {
        // forceFill (P1.4/F-P1.8-02): Outbox is fully guarded — this trusted
        // fixture builder bypasses it exactly like OutboxRecorder::stage() does.
        $outbox = new Outbox;
        $outbox->forceFill(array_merge([
            'event_id' => 'evt-'.Str::uuid7()->toString(),
            'panel' => 'default',
            'topic' => 'node-1',
            'event' => 'node.created',
            'payload' => ['node_id' => 'node-1', 'event_id' => 'evt-1'],
            'available_at' => now(),
            'status' => 'pending',
            'attempts' => 0,
        ], $attributes));
        $outbox->save();

        return $outbox;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $attributes
     */
    public static function endpoint(array $attributes = []): WebhookEndpoint
    {
        // forceFill (P1.4/F-P1.7-04): signing_secret/failure_count are excluded
        // from WebhookEndpoint's $fillable — bypassed here exactly like
        // WebhookEndpointStoreRepository::create() does.
        $endpoint = new WebhookEndpoint;
        $endpoint->forceFill(array_merge([
            'url' => 'https://hooks.test/receive',
            'events' => ['node.created'],
            'signing_secret' => 'topsecret',
            'is_active' => true,
            'failure_count' => 0,
        ], $attributes));
        $endpoint->save();

        return $endpoint;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $attributes
     */
    public static function delivery(WebhookEndpoint $endpoint, array $attributes = []): WebhookDelivery
    {
        // forceFill (P1.4/F-P1.7-04): WebhookDelivery is fully guarded —
        // bypassed here exactly like OutboxRelay::createDelivery() does.
        $delivery = new WebhookDelivery;
        $delivery->forceFill(array_merge([
            'endpoint_id' => $endpoint->getKey(),
            'event' => 'node.created',
            'payload' => ['node_id' => 'node-1', 'event_id' => 'evt-1'],
            'status' => 'pending',
            'attempts' => 0,
            'next_retry_at' => now(),
        ], $attributes));
        $delivery->save();

        return $delivery;
    }
}

FILE: packages/documents/src/Events/Comments/NodeCommentAdded.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Documents\Events\Comments;

use Illuminate\Broadcasting\Channel;
use Illuminate\Broadcasting\PrivateChannel;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Broadcasting\ShouldBroadcast;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Vaulter\Documents\Models\Comments\NodeComment;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;

/**
 * A comment was added to a node (ADR-0014 шаг 1). Broadcast on the node's private
 * collaboration channel (channel_prefix.{node_id}) — the SAME room open editors join
 * (M4b) — so a live thread updates without a poll. Dispatched by the Action AFTER the
 * insert transaction commits.
 */
final readonly class NodeCommentAdded implements ShouldBroadcast
{
    public string $eventId;

    public function __construct(
        public NodeComment $comment,
        ?string $eventId = null,
    ) {
        $this->eventId = $eventId ?? (string) Str::ulid();
    }

    /**
     * @return array<int, Channel>
     */
    public function broadcastOn(): array
    {
        return [
            new PrivateChannel(Config::channelPrefix().'.'.Identity::stringify($this->comment->node_id)),
        ];
    }

    public function broadcastAs(): string
    {
        return 'node-comment.added';
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function broadcastWith(): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $this->comment->getKey(),
            'node_id' => $this->comment->node_id,
            'parent_id' => $this->comment->parent_id,
            'body' => $this->comment->body,
            'created_at' => $this->comment->created_at,
        ];
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/documents/src/Events/Comments/NodeCommentDeleted.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Documents\Events\Comments;

use Illuminate\Broadcasting\Channel;
use Illuminate\Broadcasting\PrivateChannel;

use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;

/**
 * A node comment was permanently deleted (ADR-0014 шаг 1 — no soft-delete). Broadcast
 * on the node's private collaboration channel so open editors drop it live. The model
 * still carries its id/node_id in memory after the delete, so the payload is complete.
 */
final readonly class NodeCommentDeleted implements ShouldBroadcast
{
    public string $eventId;

    public function __construct(
        public NodeComment $comment,
        public int|string|null $actorId = null,
        ?string $eventId = null,
    ) {
        $this->eventId = $eventId ?? (string) Str::ulid();
    }

    /**
     * @return array<int, Channel>
     */
    public function broadcastOn(): array
    {
        return [
            new PrivateChannel(Config::channelPrefix().'.'.Identity::stringify($this->comment->node_id)),
        ];
    }

    public function broadcastAs(): string
    {
        return 'node-comment.deleted';
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function broadcastWith(): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $this->comment->getKey(),
            'node_id' => $this->comment->node_id,
        ];
    }
}

FILE: packages/documents/src/Events/Documents/DocumentNodeCreated.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Documents\Events\Documents;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Events\ShouldDispatchAfterCommit;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Vaulter\Documents\Models\Versions\NodeDocumentVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;

/**
 * A kind=document node was created with its initial v1 version (§2.5 / M4a). Carries

    public function __construct(
        public Node $node,

        public int|string|null $actorId = null,

    ) {
        $this->eventId = $eventId ?? (string) Str::ulid();

FILE: packages/documents/tests/Arch/PublicApiSnapshotTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

/**
 * VLT-REL P2.2 — executable freeze of vaulter-documents's public composer surface for the `0.1.0`
 * tag. Counterpart to the P2.1 SSOT (`plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/public-api-surface.md`):
 * reflects every `Vaulter\\Documents\\Contracts\**` contract into a canonical manifest and compares it to a
 * committed golden, reddening the build on any unplanned signature change (removed/renamed
 * method, narrowed type, a new contract). Same canonicalisation and AMEND procedure as
 * vaulter-core's PublicApiSnapshotTest (D5 — signatures only; config/resources/bindings
 * are frozen by the doc + `.claude/rules/public-api.md`, not reflection). `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT`
 * is env-gated and NEVER set by CI; regenerating outside AMEND (no D#) is `[POSSIBLE-DEFECT]`.
 */
if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotType')) {
    /**
     * Normalise a reflection type to a canonical, deterministic string. Union and
     * intersection members are sorted so a source-level reorder (not a BC change) does
     * not redden the build. Non-builtin types carry a leading `\` for an unambiguous FQCN.
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotType(?ReflectionType $type): string
    {
        if ($type === null) {
            return '';
        }

        if ($type instanceof ReflectionUnionType) {
            $parts = array_map('vaulterSnapshotType', $type->getTypes());
            sort($parts);

            return implode('|', $parts);
        }

        if ($type instanceof ReflectionIntersectionType) {
            $parts = array_map('vaulterSnapshotType', $type->getTypes());
            sort($parts);

            return implode('&', $parts);
        }

        /** @var ReflectionNamedType $type */
        $name = $type->getName();
        $lower = strtolower($name);
        $prefix = ($type->allowsNull() && $lower !== 'null' && $lower !== 'mixed') ? '?' : '';

        return $prefix.($type->isBuiltin() ? '' : '\\').$name;
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotValue')) {
    /**
     * Compact, deterministic rendering of a parameter default value (scalars, null,
     * shallow arrays — the only shapes contract defaults take).
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotValue(mixed $value): string
    {
        if ($value === null) {
            return 'null';
        }

        if (is_bool($value)) {
            return $value ? 'true' : 'false';
        }

        if (is_string($value)) {
            return "'".$value."'";
        }

        if (is_array($value)) {
            return '['.implode(', ', array_map('vaulterSnapshotValue', $value)).']';
        }

        if (is_int($value) || is_float($value)) {
            return (string) $value;
        }

        return get_debug_type($value);
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotMethod')) {
    /** Serialise one public method to `name(type $p = default, …): return`. */
    function vaulterSnapshotMethod(ReflectionMethod $method): string
    {
        $params = [];

        foreach ($method->getParameters() as $param) {
            $piece = vaulterSnapshotType($param->getType());
            $piece .= ' ';
            $piece .= $param->isPassedByReference() ? '&' : '';
            $piece .= $param->isVariadic() ? '...' : '';
            $piece .= '$'.$param->getName();

            if ($param->isDefaultValueAvailable()) {
                $piece .= ' = '.($param->isDefaultValueConstant()
                    ? (string) $param->getDefaultValueConstantName()
                    : vaulterSnapshotValue($param->getDefaultValue()));
            }

            $params[] = trim($piece);
        }

        $return = vaulterSnapshotType($method->getReturnType());

        return $method->getName().'('.implode(', ', $params).')'.($return !== '' ? ': '.$return : '');
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotInterface')) {
    /**
     * Serialise one contract: FQCN header + its public, non-`@internal` methods (sorted).
     *
     * @param class-string $fqcn
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotInterface(string $fqcn): string
    {
        $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
        $lines = [];

        foreach ($reflection->getMethods(ReflectionMethod::IS_PUBLIC) as $method) {
            $doc = $method->getDocComment();

            if ($doc !== false && str_contains($doc, '@internal')) {
                continue;
            }

            $lines[] = '  '.vaulterSnapshotMethod($method);
        }

        sort($lines);

        return $fqcn."\n".implode("\n", $lines);
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotContracts')) {
    /**
     * Discover every `*\Contracts\**` interface under a package src tree and map each
     * file to its FQCN (PSR-4: basename === declared type). `@internal`-marked types are
     * dropped from the frozen set.
     *
     * @return list<class-string> sorted FQCNs
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotContracts(string $contractsDir, string $namespaceRoot): array
    {
        if (!is_dir($contractsDir)) {
            return [];
        }

        /** @var Iterator<SplFileInfo> $files */
        $files = new RegexIterator(
            new RecursiveIteratorIterator(new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($contractsDir, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS)),
            '/\.php$/',
        );

        $fqcns = [];

        foreach ($files as $file) {
            $relative = substr(str_replace($contractsDir.'/', '', $file->getPathname()), 0, -4);
            $fqcn = $namespaceRoot.'\\'.str_replace('/', '\\', $relative);

            // Per-branch interface_exists()/class_exists() narrow $fqcn to class-string
            // (an OR of the two does not), so ReflectionClass type-checks without a
            // suppression. Contracts are interfaces by invariant (arch 'contracts are
            // interfaces'); the class branch keeps discovery honest if that ever slips.
            if (interface_exists($fqcn)) {
                $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
            } elseif (class_exists($fqcn)) {
                $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
            } else {
                continue;
            }

            $doc = $reflection->getDocComment();

            if ($doc !== false && str_contains($doc, '@internal')) {
                continue;
            }

            $fqcns[] = $fqcn;
        }

        sort($fqcns);

        return array_values(array_unique($fqcns));
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotFacade')) {
    /** Freeze the facade by its `@method` docblock (surface over the resolved manager). */
    function vaulterSnapshotFacade(string $facadeFqcn): string
    {
        $doc = (new ReflectionClass($facadeFqcn))->getDocComment() ?: '';
        preg_match_all('/@method\s+(.+)$/m', $doc, $matches);

        $methods = array_map(
            static fn (string $line): string => trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $line)),
            $matches[1],
        );

        sort($methods);

        return 'FACADE '.$facadeFqcn."\n".implode("\n", array_map(
            static fn (string $m): string => '  '.$m,
            $methods,
        ));
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotAssert')) {
    /**
     * Shared gate body: compare the freshly reflected manifest against the committed
     * golden, or regenerate it under the env-gated `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT` escape hatch.
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotAssert(string $manifest, string $goldenPath): void
    {
        if (getenv('UPDATE_SNAPSHOT') === '1') {
            @mkdir(dirname($goldenPath), 0o755, true);
            file_put_contents($goldenPath, $manifest);
            expect(is_file($goldenPath))->toBeTrue();

            return;
        }

        expect(is_file($goldenPath))->toBeTrue();

        expect($manifest)->toBe(
            file_get_contents($goldenPath),
            'Public API surface changed. If this is INTENTIONAL and ADDITIVE, follow AMEND '
            .'(plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/AMEND.md): regenerate the golden with UPDATE_SNAPSHOT=1, '
            .'add a D#, sync plan.md §7 Contracts, ship a CHANGELOG MINOR. If it is BREAKING '
            .'(removed/renamed method, narrowed type), that is a SemVer decision — escalate to the owner.',
        );
    }
}

test('vaulter-documents public API surface matches the frozen golden (VLT-REL P2.2)', function (): void {
    $contracts = vaulterSnapshotContracts(__DIR__.'/../../src/Contracts', 'Vaulter\Documents\Contracts');

    // Discovery must find the enumerated surface — a scan that silently finds nothing
    // would make the whole freeze an inert no-op.
    expect($contracts)->not->toBe([]);

    $blocks = array_map('vaulterSnapshotInterface', $contracts);

    foreach (vaulterSnapshotContracts(__DIR__.'/../../src/Events', 'Vaulter\Documents\Events') as $eventClass) {
        $reflection = new ReflectionClass($eventClass);
        $properties = [];
        foreach ($reflection->getProperties(ReflectionProperty::IS_PUBLIC) as $property) {
            $properties[] = '  '.($property->isReadOnly() ? 'readonly ' : '')
                .vaulterSnapshotType($property->getType()).' $'.$property->getName();
        }
        sort($properties);
        $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface($eventClass)."\n".implode("\n", $properties);
    }

    vaulterSnapshotAssert(implode("\n\n", $blocks)."\n", __DIR__.'/__snapshots__/public-api.txt');
});

FILE: packages/documents/tests/TestCase.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Documents\Tests;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Orchestra\Testbench\TestCase as OrchestraTestCase;
use Vaulter\Documents\VaulterDocumentsServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\Drive\VaulterDriveServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\Media\VaulterMediaServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\VaulterServiceProvider;

abstract class TestCase extends OrchestraTestCase
{
    /**
     * @param  Application              $app
     * @return array<int, class-string>
     */
    protected function getPackageProviders($app): array
    {
        return [
            // Core owns the schema (v_nodes/v_storages/... + v_node_comments/
            // v_node_document_versions), the Node/Storage models, Access/Sharing/
            // Activity and Support\Config.
            VaulterServiceProvider::class,
            // The document-node tests build their Storage fixtures through
            // Vaulter::for() (a Drive handle) — documents requires only core in
            // production (T6), but its OWN test suite needs drive installed to
            // create those fixtures. Document content itself is never CAS-committed,
            // but DriveContext eagerly constructs ALL Drive Actions (incl.
            // UploadFileAction) on build, which needs the Contracts\Cas\BlobStore
            // seam bound — so media is a transitive test-only need too.
            VaulterDriveServiceProvider::class,
            VaulterMediaServiceProvider::class,
            // This package binds the Documents behaviour: the TipTap/Univer format
            // drivers (added to core's registry), the collaboration
            // manager/broadcast channel, the document routes and the
            // Documents-event Activity/outbox subscribers. The registry engine
            // itself and the NodeHandlerResolver seam are core-owned (P0.4/D10) —
            // VaulterServiceProvider (core) binds them, not this provider.
            VaulterDocumentsServiceProvider::class,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param Application $app
     */
    protected function defineEnvironment($app): void
    {
        $app['config']->set('database.default', 'testing');
        $app['config']->set('database.connections.testing', $this->suiteDatabaseConnection());

        // The package's `vaulter-public`/`vaulter-uploads` named rate limiters
        // (P1.3 / F-P1.8-01) are backed by the Laravel cache. Testbench's own
        // default cache store is 'database', which needs a `cache` table this
        // suite's migrations never create — pin the in-memory 'array' store so a
        // throttled route (the collab webhooks) never 500s on a missing table.
        $app['config']->set('cache.default', 'array');

        // Drop the route middleware group so tests do not depend on the host's
        // 'api' group definition; authorization is exercised via the resolver.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.middleware', []);

        // The Documents suite runs OPEN under the env-gated NullRoleDriver (non-prod
        // only, H.2 / D6″): every actor gets full access so the tests exercise pure
        // document mechanics without an access-control lane.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.drivers.permission', 'null');

        // Pin the auto-increment strategy to bigint — the Documents tests assert
        // integer keys on the Storage→Node chain they build.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.ids.default', 'bigint');
    }

    protected function defineDatabaseMigrations(): void
    {
        // The whole v_* schema (incl. v_node_comments/v_node_document_versions) is
        // owned by core; load its migrations through the path-repo symlink Composer
        // places in this package's vendor dir.
        $this->loadMigrationsFrom(__DIR__.'/../vendor/axioma-studio/vaulter-core/database/migrations');
    }

    /**
     * The connection the whole suite runs on. Default: in-memory SQLite. Opt INTO
     * Postgres by exporting DB_CONNECTION=pgsql (plus the DB_* coordinates): the
     * dedicated pgsql CI lane exercises the yjs_state bytea NUL-safe round-trip and
     * the v_activity/v_node_document_versions jsonb gin(jsonb_path_ops) indexes that
     * SQLite cannot model.
     *
     * @return array<string, string|bool|null>
     */
    protected function suiteDatabaseConnection(): array
    {
        if (env('DB_CONNECTION') !== 'pgsql') {
            return [
                'driver' => 'sqlite',
                'database' => ':memory:',
                'prefix' => '',
            ];
        }

        return [
            'driver' => 'pgsql',
            'host' => (string) env('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1'),
            'port' => (string) env('DB_PORT', '5432'),
            'database' => (string) env('DB_DATABASE', 'vaulter_test'),
            'username' => (string) env('DB_USERNAME', 'vaulter'),
            'password' => (string) env('DB_PASSWORD', ''),
            'charset' => 'utf8',
            'prefix' => '',
            'prefix_indexes' => true,
            'search_path' => 'public',
            'sslmode' => 'prefer',
        ];
    }
}

FILE: packages/drive/src/Actions/RenameNodeAction.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\RenameNodeData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeRenamed;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;

/**
 * Rename a node in place (§2.2), subject to the sibling-name conflict check at
 * its current parent (the node itself is excluded from the check).
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class RenameNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    public function execute(RenameNodeData $data): Node
    {

        $node = $data->node;
        $fromName = $node->name;

        if ($fromName === $data->name) {
            return $node;
        }

        $exists = $this->nodes->siblingNameExists(
            storage: $node->storage,
            parentId: $node->parent_id,
            name: $data->name,
            ignoreId: $node->id,
        );

        if ($exists) {
            throw NodeNameConflictException::forName(name: $data->name, parentId: $node->parent_id);
        }

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $fromName, $data): Node {
            $renamed = $this->store->rename(node: $node, name: $data->name);

            event(new NodeRenamed(
                node: $renamed,
                fromName: $fromName,
                toName: $data->name,
                actorId: $data->actorId,
            ));

            return $renamed;
        });
    }
}

FILE: packages/drive/src/Actions/RestoreNodeAction.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeRestored;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * Restore a trashed node and its subtree (§2.10). Clears the trashed triplet
 * across the bounded subtree in one bulk UPDATE. Two deterministic destination
 * rules (§2.10 restore-collision):
 *   - if the original parent is gone (deleted / itself trashed), re-home the node
 *     to the storage root (parent_id = null) rather than dangling it;
 *   - if a LIVE sibling now owns the node's name at the resolved destination,
 *     throw NodeNameConflictException — the caller renames then retries.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class RestoreNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    public function execute(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {

        if ($node->trashed_at === null) {
            return $node;
        }

        $destinationParentId = $this->resolveDestinationParent($node);

        if ($this->liveSiblingOwnsName(node: $node, parentId: $destinationParentId)) {
            throw NodeNameConflictException::forName(name: $node->name, parentId: $destinationParentId);
        }

        $ids = $this->nodes->subtree($node)->modelKeys();

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $ids, $destinationParentId, $actorId): Node {
            // Re-home the subtree root first (so the cleared triplet lands on a
            // node already hung at its restored destination), then clear trash.
            if ($node->parent_id !== $destinationParentId) {
                $this->store->move(node: $node, parentId: $destinationParentId);
            }

            $this->store->restoreSubtree(collect($ids));

            $fresh = $node->refresh();

            event(new NodeRestored(node: $fresh, actorId: $actorId));

            return $fresh;
        });
    }

    /**
     * The destination parent for the restored node: its recorded original parent
     * when that parent is still live, else the storage root (null).
     */
    private function resolveDestinationParent(Node $node): int|string|null
    {
        $originalParentId = $node->trashed_from_parent_id ?? $node->parent_id;

        // No recorded parent (or it was the very top) → the storage root node.
        $rootId = $node->storage->root_node_id;

        if ($originalParentId === null) {
            return $rootId;
        }

        $model = Config::nodeModel();

        $parentIsLive = $model::query()
            ->whereKey($originalParentId)
            ->whereNull('trashed_at')
            ->whereNull('deleted_at')
            ->exists();

        // The original home vanished → re-home to root deterministically.
        return $parentIsLive ? $originalParentId : $rootId;
    }

    private function liveSiblingOwnsName(Node $node, int|string|null $parentId): bool
    {
        return $this->nodes->siblingNameExists(
            storage: $node->storage,
            parentId: $parentId,
            name: $node->name,
            ignoreId: $node->id,
        );
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/drive/src/Actions/TrashNodeAction.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeTrashed;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;

/**
 * Soft-trash a node and its whole subtree atomically (§2.10 / D17). The trashed
 * triplet (trashed_at, trashed_by, and trashed_from_parent_id on the root) is
 * stamped across the bounded subtree in one bulk UPDATE; the sibling-unique index
 * excludes trashed rows so the name frees up for a new sibling.
 *
 * used_bytes is NOT decremented — trashed bytes still occupy storage until
 * forceDelete (§1.5: quota counts what is physically retained, and a restore must
 * bring the bytes back). The decrement happens only in ForceDeleteNodeAction.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class TrashNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    public function execute(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {

        if ($node->trashed_at !== null) {
            return $node;
        }

        $ids = $this->nodes->subtree($node)->modelKeys();

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $ids, $actorId): Node {
            $this->store->trashSubtree(
                ids: collect($ids),
                root: $node,
                actorId: $actorId,
                at: now(),
            );

            $fresh = $node->refresh();

            event(new NodeTrashed(node: $fresh, actorId: $actorId));

            return $fresh;
        });
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/drive/src/Events/FileUploaded.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Events;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Events\ShouldDispatchAfterCommit;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;

/**
 * A brand-new file node was uploaded — first version committed against a
 * (possibly deduplicated) blob (§2.4). A subsequent upload onto the same node

    public string $eventId;

    public function __construct(
        public Node $node,

        public int|string|null $actorId = null,

    ) {
        $this->eventId = $eventId ?? (string) Str::ulid();

FILE: packages/drive/src/Events/NodeForceDeleted.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Events;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Events\ShouldDispatchAfterCommit;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * A node and its subtree were permanently soft-deleted (deleted_at set), their
 * version↔blob edges detached so the blobs become GC-eligible (M1b GC reclaims
 * the bytes — never deleted directly here, §2.4 / §2.3). used_bytes was
 * decremented by the freed live size. The node objects are gone, so only the ids
 * and the reclaimed byte total travel with the event.
 *
 * Dispatched inside the force-delete transaction, so — like every sibling Drive
 * event — it implements ShouldDispatchAfterCommit: listeners (GC scheduling,
 * notifications) only fire once the soft-delete + edge detach is durable, and
 * never on a rolled-back attempt.
 *
 * @property list<int|string> $nodeIds
 */
final readonly class NodeForceDeleted implements ShouldDispatchAfterCommit
{
    public string $eventId;

    /**
     * @param list<int|string> $nodeIds
     */
    public function __construct(
        public int|string $storageId,
        public array $nodeIds,
        public int $freedBytes,
        public int|string|null $actorId = null,
        ?string $eventId = null,
    ) {
        $this->eventId = $eventId ?? (string) Str::ulid();
    }
}

FILE: packages/drive/src/Webhooks/RecordsDriveOutbox.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Webhooks;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Events\Dispatcher;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Throwable;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\FileUploaded;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeCopied;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeCreated;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeForceDeleted;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeMoved;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeRenamed;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeRestored;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeTrashed;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeVersionCommitted;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\StorageCreated;
use Vaulter\Enums\Webhooks\WebhookEvent;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;
use Vaulter\Webhooks\OutboxRecorder;

/**
 * The vaulter-drive half of the transactional outbox (§2.11 / D14 / M3b, P2 #5): maps
 * the Drive file/folder/upload domain events to the core {@see OutboxRecorder} public
 * seam, staging ONE slim v_outbox row per event (idempotent by event_id). The kernel
 * (Access/Sharing) events are staged by core's own RecordsOutbox; splitting the
 * subscriber by owning package is what lets core stop referencing Vaulter\Drive\Events\*
 * while the outbox payloads retain their domain fields.
 *
 * Registered by VaulterDriveServiceProvider ONLY when the 'reliability' feature is on.
 * The Drive events are ShouldDispatchAfterCommit (after-commit boundary — same
 * crash-window caveat documented on core's RecordsOutbox). RESILIENCE: every handler
 * is wrapped so a staging failure is logged and swallowed, never rolling back the
 * originating action that already committed.
 */
final readonly class RecordsDriveOutbox
{
    public function __construct(
        private OutboxRecorder $recorder,
    ) {}

    public function subscribe(Dispatcher $events): void
    {
        $events->listen(StorageCreated::class, [self::class, 'handleStorageCreated']);
        $events->listen(NodeCreated::class, [self::class, 'handleNodeCreated']);
        $events->listen(FileUploaded::class, [self::class, 'handleFileUploaded']);
        $events->listen(NodeVersionCommitted::class, [self::class, 'handleVersionCommitted']);
        $events->listen(NodeMoved::class, [self::class, 'handleNodeMoved']);
        $events->listen(NodeRenamed::class, [self::class, 'handleNodeRenamed']);
        $events->listen(NodeCopied::class, [self::class, 'handleNodeCopied']);
        $events->listen(NodeTrashed::class, [self::class, 'handleNodeTrashed']);
        $events->listen(NodeRestored::class, [self::class, 'handleNodeRestored']);
        $events->listen(NodeForceDeleted::class, [self::class, 'handleNodeForceDeleted']);
    }

    public function handleStorageCreated(StorageCreated $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::StorageCreated,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->storage->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->storage->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->storage->getKey()),
                'root_node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->root->getKey()),
                'name' => $event->storage->name,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), StorageCreated::class);
    }

    public function handleNodeCreated(NodeCreated $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::NodeCreated,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->node->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->storage_id),
                'parent_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->parent_id),
                'kind' => $event->node->kind->value,
                'name' => $event->node->name,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), NodeCreated::class);
    }

    public function handleFileUploaded(FileUploaded $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::FileUploaded,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->node->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->storage_id),
                'version_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->version->getKey()),
                'name' => $event->node->name,
                'size_bytes' => $event->version->size_bytes,
                'mime_type' => $event->version->mime_type,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), FileUploaded::class);
    }

    public function handleVersionCommitted(NodeVersionCommitted $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::VersionCreated,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->node->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->storage_id),
                'version_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->version->getKey()),
                'version_no' => $event->versionNo,
                'size_bytes' => $event->version->size_bytes,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), NodeVersionCommitted::class);
    }

    public function handleNodeMoved(NodeMoved $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::NodeMoved,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->node->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->storage_id),
                'from_parent_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->fromParentId),
                'to_parent_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->toParentId),
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), NodeMoved::class);
    }

    public function handleNodeRenamed(NodeRenamed $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::NodeRenamed,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->node->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->storage_id),
                'from' => $event->fromName,
                'to' => $event->toName,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), NodeRenamed::class);
    }

    public function handleNodeCopied(NodeCopied $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::NodeCopied,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->copy->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->copy->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->copy->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->copy->storage_id),
                'source_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->source->getKey()),
                'name' => $event->copy->name,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), NodeCopied::class);
    }

    public function handleNodeTrashed(NodeTrashed $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::NodeTrashed,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->node->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->storage_id),
                'name' => $event->node->name,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), NodeTrashed::class);
    }

    public function handleNodeRestored(NodeRestored $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::NodeRestored,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: $event->node->panel,
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
            payload: [
                'node_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->getKey()),
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->storage_id),
                'parent_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->node->parent_id),
                'name' => $event->node->name,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), NodeRestored::class);
    }

    /**
     * The node objects are gone after a force-delete, so the slim payload carries
     * the deleted ids + freed bytes (the relay still fans this out so a host can
     * reconcile its own mirror). The panel is the default panel — the storage is the
     * only surviving scope and the event does not carry it.
     */
    public function handleNodeForceDeleted(NodeForceDeleted $event): void
    {
        $this->safely(fn (): mixed => $this->recorder->stage(
            event: WebhookEvent::NodeForceDeleted,
            eventId: $event->eventId,
            panel: 'default',
            topic: $this->recorder->id($event->storageId),
            payload: [
                'storage_id' => $this->recorder->id($event->storageId),
                'node_ids' => array_map(Identity::stringify(...), $event->nodeIds),
                'freed_bytes' => $event->freedBytes,
            ],
            actorId: $event->actorId,
        ), NodeForceDeleted::class);
    }

    /**
     * Run a recorder call, swallowing + logging any failure. Staging must never break
     * the originating action; the event already committed (after-commit boundary), so
     * a missed outbox row is a logged degradation, not a transaction failure.
     *
     * @param callable(): mixed $record
     */
    private function safely(callable $record, string $event): void
    {
        try {
            $record();
        } catch (Throwable $exception) {
            Log::warning('Vaulter outbox staging failed; the originating action is unaffected.', [
                'event' => $event,
                'exception' => $exception->getMessage(),
            ]);
        }
    }
}

END FILE

FILE: packages/drive/tests/Arch/PublicApiSnapshotTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

/**
 * VLT-REL P2.2 — executable freeze of vaulter-drive's public composer surface for the `0.1.0`
 * tag. Counterpart to the P2.1 SSOT (`plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/public-api-surface.md`):
 * reflects every `Vaulter\\Drive\\Contracts\**` contract into a canonical manifest and compares it to a
 * committed golden, reddening the build on any unplanned signature change (removed/renamed
 * method, narrowed type, a new contract). Same canonicalisation and AMEND procedure as
 * vaulter-core's PublicApiSnapshotTest (D5 — signatures only; config/resources/bindings
 * are frozen by the doc + `.claude/rules/public-api.md`, not reflection). `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT`
 * is env-gated and NEVER set by CI; regenerating outside AMEND (no D#) is `[POSSIBLE-DEFECT]`.
 */
if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotType')) {
    /**
     * Normalise a reflection type to a canonical, deterministic string. Union and
     * intersection members are sorted so a source-level reorder (not a BC change) does
     * not redden the build. Non-builtin types carry a leading `\` for an unambiguous FQCN.
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotType(?ReflectionType $type): string
    {
        if ($type === null) {
            return '';
        }

        if ($type instanceof ReflectionUnionType) {
            $parts = array_map('vaulterSnapshotType', $type->getTypes());
            sort($parts);

            return implode('|', $parts);
        }

        if ($type instanceof ReflectionIntersectionType) {
            $parts = array_map('vaulterSnapshotType', $type->getTypes());
            sort($parts);

            return implode('&', $parts);
        }

        /** @var ReflectionNamedType $type */
        $name = $type->getName();
        $lower = strtolower($name);
        $prefix = ($type->allowsNull() && $lower !== 'null' && $lower !== 'mixed') ? '?' : '';

        return $prefix.($type->isBuiltin() ? '' : '\\').$name;
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotValue')) {
    /**
     * Compact, deterministic rendering of a parameter default value (scalars, null,
     * shallow arrays — the only shapes contract defaults take).
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotValue(mixed $value): string
    {
        if ($value === null) {
            return 'null';
        }

        if (is_bool($value)) {
            return $value ? 'true' : 'false';
        }

        if (is_string($value)) {
            return "'".$value."'";
        }

        if (is_array($value)) {
            return '['.implode(', ', array_map('vaulterSnapshotValue', $value)).']';
        }

        if (is_int($value) || is_float($value)) {
            return (string) $value;
        }

        return get_debug_type($value);
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotMethod')) {
    /** Serialise one public method to `name(type $p = default, …): return`. */
    function vaulterSnapshotMethod(ReflectionMethod $method): string
    {
        $params = [];

        foreach ($method->getParameters() as $param) {
            $piece = vaulterSnapshotType($param->getType());
            $piece .= ' ';
            $piece .= $param->isPassedByReference() ? '&' : '';
            $piece .= $param->isVariadic() ? '...' : '';
            $piece .= '$'.$param->getName();

            if ($param->isDefaultValueAvailable()) {
                $piece .= ' = '.($param->isDefaultValueConstant()
                    ? (string) $param->getDefaultValueConstantName()
                    : vaulterSnapshotValue($param->getDefaultValue()));
            }

            $params[] = trim($piece);
        }

        $return = vaulterSnapshotType($method->getReturnType());

        return $method->getName().'('.implode(', ', $params).')'.($return !== '' ? ': '.$return : '');
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotInterface')) {
    /**
     * Serialise one contract: FQCN header + its public, non-`@internal` methods (sorted).
     *
     * @param class-string $fqcn
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotInterface(string $fqcn): string
    {
        $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
        $lines = [];

        foreach ($reflection->getMethods(ReflectionMethod::IS_PUBLIC) as $method) {
            $doc = $method->getDocComment();

            if ($doc !== false && str_contains($doc, '@internal')) {
                continue;
            }

            $lines[] = '  '.vaulterSnapshotMethod($method);
        }

        sort($lines);

        return $fqcn."\n".implode("\n", $lines);
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotContracts')) {
    /**
     * Discover every `*\Contracts\**` interface under a package src tree and map each
     * file to its FQCN (PSR-4: basename === declared type). `@internal`-marked types are
     * dropped from the frozen set.
     *
     * @return list<class-string> sorted FQCNs
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotContracts(string $contractsDir, string $namespaceRoot): array
    {
        if (!is_dir($contractsDir)) {
            return [];
        }

        /** @var Iterator<SplFileInfo> $files */
        $files = new RegexIterator(
            new RecursiveIteratorIterator(new RecursiveDirectoryIterator($contractsDir, FilesystemIterator::SKIP_DOTS)),
            '/\.php$/',
        );

        $fqcns = [];

        foreach ($files as $file) {
            $relative = substr(str_replace($contractsDir.'/', '', $file->getPathname()), 0, -4);
            $fqcn = $namespaceRoot.'\\'.str_replace('/', '\\', $relative);

            // Per-branch interface_exists()/class_exists() narrow $fqcn to class-string
            // (an OR of the two does not), so ReflectionClass type-checks without a
            // suppression. Contracts are interfaces by invariant (arch 'contracts are
            // interfaces'); the class branch keeps discovery honest if that ever slips.
            if (interface_exists($fqcn)) {
                $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
            } elseif (class_exists($fqcn)) {
                $reflection = new ReflectionClass($fqcn);
            } else {
                continue;
            }

            $doc = $reflection->getDocComment();

            if ($doc !== false && str_contains($doc, '@internal')) {
                continue;
            }

            $fqcns[] = $fqcn;
        }

        sort($fqcns);

        return array_values(array_unique($fqcns));
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotFacade')) {
    /** Freeze the facade by its `@method` docblock (surface over the resolved manager). */
    function vaulterSnapshotFacade(string $facadeFqcn): string
    {
        $doc = (new ReflectionClass($facadeFqcn))->getDocComment() ?: '';
        preg_match_all('/@method\s+(.+)$/m', $doc, $matches);

        $methods = array_map(
            static fn (string $line): string => trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $line)),
            $matches[1],
        );

        sort($methods);

        return 'FACADE '.$facadeFqcn."\n".implode("\n", array_map(
            static fn (string $m): string => '  '.$m,
            $methods,
        ));
    }
}

if (!function_exists('vaulterSnapshotAssert')) {
    /**
     * Shared gate body: compare the freshly reflected manifest against the committed
     * golden, or regenerate it under the env-gated `UPDATE_SNAPSHOT` escape hatch.
     */
    function vaulterSnapshotAssert(string $manifest, string $goldenPath): void
    {
        if (getenv('UPDATE_SNAPSHOT') === '1') {
            @mkdir(dirname($goldenPath), 0o755, true);
            file_put_contents($goldenPath, $manifest);
            expect(is_file($goldenPath))->toBeTrue();

            return;
        }

        expect(is_file($goldenPath))->toBeTrue();

        expect($manifest)->toBe(
            file_get_contents($goldenPath),
            'Public API surface changed. If this is INTENTIONAL and ADDITIVE, follow AMEND '
            .'(plans/archive/VLT-REL/root/contracts/AMEND.md): regenerate the golden with UPDATE_SNAPSHOT=1, '
            .'add a D#, sync plan.md §7 Contracts, ship a CHANGELOG MINOR. If it is BREAKING '
            .'(removed/renamed method, narrowed type), that is a SemVer decision — escalate to the owner.',
        );
    }
}

test('vaulter-drive public API surface matches the frozen golden (VLT-REL P2.2)', function (): void {
    $contracts = vaulterSnapshotContracts(__DIR__.'/../../src/Contracts', 'Vaulter\Drive\Contracts');

    // Discovery must find the enumerated surface — a scan that silently finds nothing
    // would make the whole freeze an inert no-op.
    expect($contracts)->not->toBe([]);

    $blocks = array_map('vaulterSnapshotInterface', $contracts);

    foreach (vaulterSnapshotContracts(__DIR__.'/../../src/Events', 'Vaulter\Drive\Events') as $eventClass) {
        $reflection = new ReflectionClass($eventClass);
        $properties = [];
        foreach ($reflection->getProperties(ReflectionProperty::IS_PUBLIC) as $property) {
            $properties[] = '  '.($property->isReadOnly() ? 'readonly ' : '')
                .vaulterSnapshotType($property->getType()).' $'.$property->getName();
        }
        sort($properties);
        $blocks[] = vaulterSnapshotInterface($eventClass)."\n".implode("\n", $properties);
    }

    vaulterSnapshotAssert(implode("\n\n", $blocks)."\n", __DIR__.'/__snapshots__/public-api.txt');
});

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\Uploads\UploadInitData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Enums\Uploads\UploadProtocol;
use Vaulter\Drive\StorageManager;
use Vaulter\Drive\Tests\Uploads\UploadTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Drive\Uploads\UploadSessionManager;
use Vaulter\Enums\Activity\ActivityVerb;
use Vaulter\Enums\Webhooks\WebhookEvent;
use Vaulter\Models\Activity\Activity;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\Outbox;
use Vaulter\Testing\FakeMetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Tests\Access\AccessTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
});

it('purge-trash reports zero subtrees when nothing qualifies', function (): void {
    Artisan::call('vaulter:purge-trash');

    expect(Artisan::output())->toContain('Purged 0 trashed subtrees.');
});

it('purge-trash purges on the cutoff day but spares one day newer', function (): void {
    $this->travelTo(Carbon::parse('2026-02-10 12:00:00'));
    $storage = AccessTestHelpers::storage();

    $onCutoff = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, 'on-cutoff');
    $onCutoff->forceFill(['trashed_at' => Carbon::parse('2026-02-09 12:00:00')])->save();

    $newer = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, 'newer');
    $newer->forceFill(['trashed_at' => Carbon::parse('2026-02-10 00:00:01')])->save();

    Artisan::call('vaulter:purge-trash', ['--days' => '1']);
    $output = Artisan::output();

    expect($output)->toContain('Purging nodes trashed on or before 2026-02-09 12:00:00 (retention: 1 day).')
        ->and($output)->toContain('Purged 1 trashed subtree.')
        ->and(Node::query()->whereKey($onCutoff->getKey())->exists())->toBeFalse()
        ->and(Node::query()->whereKey($newer->getKey())->exists())->toBeTrue();
});

it('purge-trash pluralizes multiple purged roots', function (): void {
    $this->travelTo(Carbon::parse('2026-03-01 08:00:00'));
    $storage = AccessTestHelpers::storage();

    foreach (['a', 'b'] as $name) {
        $node = AccessTestHelpers::folder($storage, null, $name);
        $node->forceFill(['trashed_at' => Carbon::now()->subDays(3)])->save();
    }

    Artisan::call('vaulter:purge-trash', ['--days' => '1']);

    expect(Artisan::output())->toContain('Purged 2 trashed subtrees.');
});

it('records sweep counters for the materialised stale batch size', function (): void {
    config()->set('vaulter.observability.enabled', true);
    config()->set('vaulter.observability.recorder', FakeMetricsRecorder::class);
    app()->forgetInstance(MetricsRecorder::class);

    $storage = UploadTestHelpers::storage();
    foreach (['one', 'two'] as $name) {
        $session = UploadTestHelpers::session($storage, targetName: $name.'.bin');
        $session->forceFill(['expires_at' => Carbon::now()->subHour()])->save();
    }

    Artisan::call('vaulter:sweep-uploads');

    $recorder = app(MetricsRecorder::class);
    $recorder->assertHistogram(name: 'vaulter_sweep_batch_rows');
    $recorder->assertCounter(name: 'vaulter_sweep_expired_total');
    $recorder->assertCounter(name: 'vaulter_sweep_skipped_total');

    expect(Artisan::output())->toContain('Swept 2 stale upload sessions.');
});

it('createFolder activity context carries owner and creator ids', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    Activity::query()->delete();

    $folder = $drive->createFolder('Audited', actorId: 42);

    expect(Activity::query()->where('verb', ActivityVerb::NodeCreated->value)->sole()->context)->toEqual([
        'kind' => 'folder',
        'name' => 'Audited',
        'parent_id' => (string) $drive->root()->getKey(),
    ])
        ->and($folder->owner_id)->toEqual(42)
        ->and($folder->created_by)->toEqual(42);
});

it('move within the same parent is a no-op without NodeMoved activity', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $parent = $drive->createFolder('Parent');
    $child = $drive->createFolder('Child', $parent);

    Activity::query()->delete();
    Outbox::query()->delete();

    $drive->move($child, $parent, actorId: 17);

    expect(Activity::query()->where('verb', ActivityVerb::NodeMoved->value)->count())->toBe(0)
        ->and(Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::NodeMoved->value)->count())->toBe(0);
});

it('forceDelete on a folder subtree records zero freed bytes and skips quota adjustment', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $folder = $drive->createFolder('Empty tree');
    $used = $drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes;

    $drive->forceDelete($folder, actorId: 17);

    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe($used)
        ->and(Activity::query()->where('verb', ActivityVerb::NodeForceDeleted->value)->sole()->context)->toEqual([
            'node_ids' => [(string) $folder->getKey()],
            'freed_bytes' => 0,
        ]);
});

it('restore re-homes to the storage root when the original parent was trashed', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $parent = $drive->createFolder('Parent');
    $child = $drive->createFolder('Child', $parent);

    $drive->trash($parent);
    $drive->trash($child);

    $restored = $drive->restore($child->refresh(), actorId: 17);

    expect($restored->parent_id)->toEqual($drive->storage()->root_node_id)
        ->and(Activity::query()->where('verb', ActivityVerb::NodeRestored->value)->orderByDesc('id')->firstOrFail()->context)->toEqual([
            'name' => 'Child',
            'parent_id' => (string) $drive->storage()->root_node_id,
        ]);
});

it('upload session initiated activity carries the exact lifecycle context', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    Activity::query()->delete();

    $session = app(UploadSessionManager::class)->initiate(new UploadInitData(
        storage: $drive->storage(),
        protocol: UploadProtocol::Tus,
        targetParent: $drive->root(),
        targetName: 'ctx.bin',
        totalSizeBytes: 8,
        mimeType: null,
    ));

    $row = Activity::query()->where('verb', ActivityVerb::UploadSessionInitiated->value)->sole();

    expect($row->context)->toEqual([
        'upload_id' => $session->upload_id,
        'protocol' => 'tus',
        'target_name' => 'ctx.bin',
    ]);
});

it('force-delete outbox occurrence ids are distinct ULIDs', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    Outbox::query()->delete();

    $first = $drive->createFolder('One');
    $drive->forceDelete($first, actorId: 9);
    $firstKey = Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::NodeForceDeleted->value)->sole()->event_id;

    $second = $drive->createFolder('Two');
    $drive->forceDelete($second, actorId: 9);
    $secondKey = Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::NodeForceDeleted->value)->orderByDesc('id')->firstOrFail()->event_id;

    expect($firstKey)->not->toBe($secondKey)
        ->and(Str::isUlid($firstKey))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(Str::isUlid($secondKey))->toBeTrue();
});

it('DriveContext upload from a string uses the provided original name for the version row', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $node = $drive->upload('plain-bytes', originalName: 'from-string.txt', mimeType: 'text/plain', actorId: 3);

    expect($node->name)->toBe('from-string.txt')
        ->and($node->currentVersion()->firstOrFail()->original_name)->toBe('from-string.txt');
});

it('StorageManager root() provisions a missing root with an empty name', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $storage = $drive->storage();
    $oldRootId = $storage->root_node_id;

    $storage->forceFill(['root_node_id' => null])->save();
    Node::query()->whereKey($oldRootId)->delete();

    $root = app(StorageManager::class)->root($storage->refresh());

    expect($root->parent_id)->toBeNull()
        ->and($root->name)->toBe('')
        ->and($storage->refresh()->root_node_id)->toEqual($root->getKey());
});

FILE: packages/drive/tests/Feature/OutboxEventIdentityTest.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeRenamed;
use Vaulter\Enums\Webhooks\WebhookEvent;
use Vaulter\Models\Webhooks\Outbox;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    expect(Config::featureEnabled('reliability'))->toBeTrue();
});

it('stages three distinct occurrences for rename A to B to A to B', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->createFolder('A');

    foreach (['B', 'A', 'B'] as $name) {
        $drive->rename($node->refresh(), $name);
    }

    $rows = Outbox::query()->where('topic', (string) $node->getKey())
        ->where('event', WebhookEvent::NodeRenamed->value)->orderBy('id')->get();

    expect($rows)->toHaveCount(3)
        ->and($rows->pluck('event_id')->unique())->toHaveCount(3)
        ->and($rows->pluck('payload.to')->all())->toBe(['B', 'A', 'B']);

    foreach ($rows as $row) {
        expect(Str::isUlid($row->event_id))->toBeTrue()
            ->and(strlen($row->event_id))->toBe(26)
            ->and($row->payload['event_id'])->toBe($row->event_id);
    }
});

it('stages three distinct occurrences for trash restore trash', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->createFolder('Lifecycle');

    $drive->trash($node);
    $drive->restore($node->refresh());
    $drive->trash($node->refresh());

    $rows = Outbox::query()->where('topic', (string) $node->getKey())
        ->whereIn('event', [WebhookEvent::NodeTrashed->value, WebhookEvent::NodeRestored->value])
        ->orderBy('id')->get();

    expect($rows)->toHaveCount(3)
        ->and($rows->pluck('event_id')->unique())->toHaveCount(3)
        ->and($rows->pluck('event')->all())->toBe([
            WebhookEvent::NodeTrashed->value,
            WebhookEvent::NodeRestored->value,
            WebhookEvent::NodeTrashed->value,
        ]);

    foreach ($rows as $row) {
        expect(Str::isUlid($row->event_id))->toBeTrue()
            ->and($row->payload['event_id'])->toBe($row->event_id);
    }
});

it('stages one row when the same event occurrence is dispatched twice', function (): void {
    $node = DriveTestHelpers::drive()->createFolder('A');
    $event = new NodeRenamed(node: $node, fromName: 'A', toName: 'B');

    event($event);
    event($event);

    $row = Outbox::query()->where('event', WebhookEvent::NodeRenamed->value)->sole();

    expect($row->event_id)->toBe($event->eventId)
        ->and($row->payload['event_id'])->toBe($event->eventId);
});

FILE: packages/drive/tests/TestCase.php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Tests;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Orchestra\Testbench\TestCase as OrchestraTestCase;
use Vaulter\Drive\VaulterDriveServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\Media\VaulterMediaServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\VaulterServiceProvider;

abstract class TestCase extends OrchestraTestCase
{
    /**
     * @param  Application              $app
     * @return array<int, class-string>
     */
    protected function getPackageProviders($app): array
    {
        return [
            // Core owns the schema (v_nodes/v_storages/v_files/... + v_upload_*),
            // the Node/Storage models, Access/Sharing/Activity and Support\Config.
            VaulterServiceProvider::class,
            // Media binds the concrete CAS engine behind the Contracts\Cas seam —
            // Drive upload/download/versioning commit through it.
            VaulterMediaServiceProvider::class,
            // This package binds the Drive behaviour: StorageManager, the DriveHandle
            // factory, the resumable-upload lifecycle, the PurgeTrash/SweepUploads
            // commands, the drive routes and the Drive-event Activity/outbox subscribers.
            VaulterDriveServiceProvider::class,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param Application $app
     */
    protected function defineEnvironment($app): void
    {
        $app['config']->set('database.default', 'testing');
        $app['config']->set('database.connections.testing', $this->suiteDatabaseConnection());

        // The package's `vaulter-public`/`vaulter-uploads` named rate limiters
        // (P1.3 / F-P1.8-01) are backed by the Laravel cache. Testbench's own
        // default cache store is 'database', which needs a `cache` table this
        // suite's migrations never create — pin the in-memory 'array' store so a
        // throttled route never 500s on a missing table.
        $app['config']->set('cache.default', 'array');

        // Drop the route middleware group so tests do not depend on the host's
        // 'api' group definition; authorization is exercised via the resolver.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.middleware', []);

        // The Drive suite runs OPEN under the env-gated NullRoleDriver (non-prod
        // only, H.2 / D6″): every actor gets full access so the tests exercise pure
        // file/folder/upload mechanics without an access-control lane.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.drivers.permission', 'null');

        // Pin the auto-increment strategy to bigint — the Drive tests assert integer
        // keys on the Storage→Node→File→Version chain they build.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.ids.default', 'bigint');
    }

    protected function defineDatabaseMigrations(): void
    {
        // The whole v_* schema (incl. v_upload_sessions/v_upload_parts) is owned by
        // core; load its migrations through the path-repo symlink Composer places in
        // this package's vendor dir.
        $this->loadMigrationsFrom(__DIR__.'/../vendor/axioma-studio/vaulter-core/database/migrations');
    }

    /**
     * The connection the whole suite runs on. Default: in-memory SQLite. Opt INTO
     * Postgres by exporting DB_CONNECTION=pgsql (plus the DB_* coordinates): the
     * dedicated pgsql CI lane exercises the real advisory-xact-lock / FOR UPDATE /
     * SERIALIZABLE critical sections behind the race-free upload-commit that SQLite
     * cannot model — every such test SKIPS unless the live driver is pgsql.
     *
     * @return array<string, string|bool|null>
     */
    protected function suiteDatabaseConnection(): array
    {
        if (env('DB_CONNECTION') !== 'pgsql') {
            return [
                'driver' => 'sqlite',
                'database' => ':memory:',
                'prefix' => '',
            ];
        }

        return [
            'driver' => 'pgsql',
            'host' => (string) env('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1'),
            'port' => (string) env('DB_PORT', '5432'),
            'database' => (string) env('DB_DATABASE', 'vaulter_test'),
            'username' => (string) env('DB_USERNAME', 'vaulter'),
            'password' => (string) env('DB_PASSWORD', ''),
            'charset' => 'utf8',
            'prefix' => '',
            'prefix_indexes' => true,
            'search_path' => 'public',
            'sslmode' => 'prefer',
        ];
    }
}

END FILE
